// ===== BEGIN FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml =====

name: CI

on:
  push:
  pull_request:

jobs:
  backend:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "21"
          cache: maven
      - run: mvn -B test

  frontend:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: frontend
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: "22"
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: frontend/package-lock.json
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm run build

// ===== END FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: Dockerfile =====

FROM maven:3.9-eclipse-temurin-21 AS build
WORKDIR /workspace
COPY pom.xml .
COPY backend ./backend
RUN mvn -B -DskipTests package

FROM eclipse-temurin:21-jre
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=build /workspace/target/jetsetgo-1.0.0.jar app.jar
EXPOSE 8080
ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75.0", "-jar", "/app/app.jar"]

// ===== END FILE: Dockerfile =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/JetSetGoApplication.java =====

package com.jetsetgo;

import org.springframework.boot.SpringApplication;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.SpringBootApplication;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.EnableScheduling;

@SpringBootApplication
@EnableScheduling
public class JetSetGoApplication {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SpringApplication.run(JetSetGoApplication.class, args);
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/JetSetGoApplication.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/DemoData.java =====

public class DemoData {
    @Bean

            if (adminEmail != null && !adminEmail.isBlank()

                users.save(new AppUser(null, adminEmail.trim().toLowerCase(),
                        passwords.encode(adminPassword), "Jet Set Go Admin", AppUser.Role.ADMIN));

    static List<Airport> seedCuratedAirports(AirportRepository airports) {
        List<Airport> curated = List.of(
                        airport("DEL", "Delhi", "Indira Gandhi International Airport", "Delhi", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("BOM", "Mumbai", "Chhatrapati Shivaji Maharaj International Airport", "Maharashtra", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("BLR", "Bengaluru", "Kempegowda International Airport", "Karnataka", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("HYD", "Hyderabad", "Rajiv Gandhi International Airport", "Telangana", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("MAA", "Chennai", "Chennai International Airport", "Tamil Nadu", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("CCU", "Kolkata", "Netaji Subhas Chandra Bose International Airport", "West Bengal", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("LKO", "Lucknow", "Chaudhary Charan Singh International Airport", "Uttar Pradesh", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("GAU", "Guwahati", "Lokpriya Gopinath Bordoloi International Airport", "Assam", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("GOI", "Goa", "Dabolim Airport", "Goa", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("GOX", "Goa", "Manohar International Airport", "Goa", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("PNQ", "Pune", "Pune Airport", "Maharashtra", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("DXB", "Dubai", "Dubai International Airport", "Dubai", "United Arab Emirates", "AE", "Asia/Dubai"),
                        airport("AUH", "Abu Dhabi", "Zayed International Airport", "Abu Dhabi", "United Arab Emirates", "AE", "Asia/Dubai"),
                        airport("SIN", "Singapore", "Singapore Changi Airport", "Singapore", "Singapore", "SG", "Asia/Singapore"),
                        airport("LHR", "London", "Heathrow Airport", "England", "United Kingdom", "GB", "Europe/London"),
                        airport("LGW", "London", "Gatwick Airport", "England", "United Kingdom", "GB", "Europe/London"),
                        airport("JFK", "New York", "John F. Kennedy International Airport", "New York", "United States", "US", "America/New_York"),
                        airport("EWR", "New York", "Newark Liberty International Airport", "New Jersey", "United States", "US", "America/New_York"),
                        airport("LGA", "New York", "LaGuardia Airport", "New York", "United States", "US", "America/New_York"),
                        airport("LAX", "Los Angeles", "Los Angeles International Airport", "California", "United States", "US", "America/Los_Angeles"),
                        airport("NRT", "Tokyo", "Narita International Airport", "Chiba", "Japan", "JP", "Asia/Tokyo"),
                        airport("HND", "Tokyo", "Tokyo Haneda Airport", "Tokyo", "Japan", "JP", "Asia/Tokyo"),
                        airport("KUL", "Kuala Lumpur", "Kuala Lumpur International Airport", "Selangor", "Malaysia", "MY", "Asia/Kuala_Lumpur"),
                        airport("AMD", "Ahmedabad", "Sardar Vallabhbhai Patel International Airport", "Gujarat", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("JAI", "Jaipur", "Jaipur International Airport", "Rajasthan", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("COK", "Kochi", "Cochin International Airport", "Kerala", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("BBI", "Bhubaneswar", "Biju Patnaik International Airport", "Odisha", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("PAT", "Patna", "Jay Prakash Narayan Airport", "Bihar", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("VNS", "Varanasi", "Lal Bahadur Shastri International Airport", "Uttar Pradesh", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("IXC", "Chandigarh", "Shaheed Bhagat Singh International Airport", "Chandigarh", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("IXD", "Allahabad", "Prayagraj Airport", "Uttar Pradesh", "India", "IN", "Asia/Kolkata"),
                        airport("BKK", "Bangkok", "Suvarnabhumi Airport", "Bangkok", "Thailand", "TH", "Asia/Bangkok"),
                        airport("DOH", "Doha", "Hamad International Airport", "Doha", "Qatar", "QA", "Asia/Qatar"),
                        airport("CDG", "Paris", "Charles de Gaulle Airport", "Île-de-France", "France", "FR", "Europe/Paris"));
        Map<String, Airport> existing = new HashMap<>();
        airports.findAllById(curated.stream().map(Airport::code).toList())
                .forEach(airport -> existing.put(airport.code(), airport));
        List<Airport> missingOrIncomplete = curated.stream()
                .filter(airport -> {
                    Airport saved = existing.get(airport.code());
                    return saved == null || isBlank(saved.countryCode()) || isBlank(saved.country())
                            || isBlank(saved.dataSource());
                })
                .toList();
        if (!missingOrIncomplete.isEmpty()) {
            airports.saveAll(missingOrIncomplete);
        }
        return missingOrIncomplete;
    }

        List<String> ids = configured.stream().map(RouteTemplate::id).toList();
        java.util.Set<String> existing = templates.findAllById(ids).stream()
                .map(RouteTemplate::id).collect(java.util.stream.Collectors.toSet());
        List<RouteTemplate> missing = configured.stream()
                .filter(template -> !existing.contains(template.id())).toList();
        if (!missing.isEmpty()) {
            templates.saveAll(missing);
        }
        return missing;

    private static RouteTemplate route(String id, String flightNumber,
                                       String originCode, String originCity,
                                       String destinationCode, String destinationCity,
                                       String departureLocalTime, int durationMinutes,
                                       String originTimeZone, String destinationTimeZone,
                                       String baseFare, List<Integer> operatingDays) {
        return new RouteTemplate(id, "JetSetGo Demonstration Air", flightNumber, originCode, originCity,
                destinationCode, destinationCity, departureLocalTime, durationMinutes, operatingDays,
                originTimeZone, destinationTimeZone, new BigDecimal(baseFare), "INR", 48,
                "Illustrative narrow-body aircraft", true);
    }

    private static boolean isBlank(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank();
    }

    private static Airport airport(String code, String city, String name, String stateOrRegion,
                                   String country, String countryCode, String timeZone) {
        return new Airport(code, city, name, stateOrRegion, country, countryCode, timeZone,
                true, "large_airport", null, null, "CURATED");
    }

    private static Flight flight(String id, String airline, String number, String originCode,
                                 String origin, String destinationCode, String destination,
                                 String originZone, String destinationZone, int dayOffset,
                                 LocalTime departure, LocalTime arrival, String price) {
        ZoneId originTimeZone = ZoneId.of(originZone);
        ZoneId destinationTimeZone = ZoneId.of(destinationZone);
        LocalDate date = LocalDate.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata")).plusDays(dayOffset);
        ZonedDateTime takeoff = date.atTime(departure).atZone(originTimeZone);
        ZonedDateTime landing = date.atTime(arrival).atZone(destinationTimeZone);
        if (!landing.toInstant().isAfter(takeoff.toInstant())) {
            landing = landing.plusDays(1);
        }
        return new Flight(id, airline, number, originCode, origin, destinationCode, destination,
                takeoff.toInstant(), landing.toInstant(), new BigDecimal(price), "INR", 48, "SCHEDULED", "DEMO",
                originZone, destinationZone);
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/DemoData.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.config;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.core.authority.SimpleGrantedAuthority;
import org.springframework.security.core.context.SecurityContextHolder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.filter.OncePerRequestFilter;

import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.JwtException;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

@Component
public class JwtAuthenticationFilter extends OncePerRequestFilter {
    private final JwtService jwtService;

    public JwtAuthenticationFilter(JwtService jwtService) {
        this.jwtService = jwtService;
    }

    @Override
    protected void doFilterInternal(HttpServletRequest request, HttpServletResponse response,
                                    FilterChain chain) throws ServletException, IOException {
        String header = request.getHeader("Authorization");
        if (header != null && header.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            try {
                Claims claims = jwtService.parse(header.substring(7));
                String role = claims.get("role", String.class);
                var authentication = new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(
                        claims.getSubject(), null,
                        List.of(new SimpleGrantedAuthority("ROLE_" + role)));
                SecurityContextHolder.getContext().setAuthentication(authentication);
            } catch (JwtException | IllegalArgumentException ex) {
                SecurityContextHolder.clearContext();
            }
        }
        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/JwtAuthenticationFilter.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/JwtService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.config;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;

import javax.crypto.SecretKey;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.AppUser;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Jwts;
import io.jsonwebtoken.security.Keys;

@Service
public class JwtService {
    private final SecretKey key;
    private final long expirationHours;

    public JwtService(@Value("${app.jwt.secret}") String secret,
                      @Value("${app.jwt.expiration-hours}") long expirationHours) {
        if (secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length < 32) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("JWT_SECRET must be at least 32 bytes");
        }
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        this.expirationHours = expirationHours;
    }

    public String issue(AppUser user) {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        return Jwts.builder()
                .subject(user.id())
                .claim("email", user.email())
                .claim("role", user.role().name())
                .issuedAt(java.util.Date.from(now))
                .expiration(java.util.Date.from(now.plus(expirationHours, ChronoUnit.HOURS)))
                .signWith(key)
                .compact();
    }

    public Claims parse(String token) {
        return Jwts.parser().verifyWith(key).build().parseSignedClaims(token).getPayload();
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/JwtService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/SecurityConfig.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.config;

import java.util.Arrays;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
import org.springframework.security.config.annotation.web.builders.HttpSecurity;
import org.springframework.security.config.http.SessionCreationPolicy;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.security.web.SecurityFilterChain;
import org.springframework.security.web.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfiguration;
import org.springframework.web.cors.CorsConfigurationSource;
import org.springframework.web.cors.UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource;

@Configuration
public class SecurityConfig {
    @Bean
    SecurityFilterChain securityFilterChain(HttpSecurity http, JwtAuthenticationFilter jwtFilter)
            throws Exception {
        return http
                .csrf(csrf -> csrf.disable())
                .cors(cors -> {})
                .sessionManagement(session -> session.sessionCreationPolicy(SessionCreationPolicy.STATELESS))
                .authorizeHttpRequests(auth -> auth
                        .requestMatchers("/api/health", "/error").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/api/auth/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.GET, "/api/airports", "/api/airports/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.GET, "/api/payments/config").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers(HttpMethod.GET, "/api/flights/**").permitAll()
                        .requestMatchers("/api/admin/**").hasRole("ADMIN")
                        .anyRequest().authenticated())
                .addFilterBefore(jwtFilter, UsernamePasswordAuthenticationFilter.class)
                .build();
    }

    @Bean
    CorsConfigurationSource corsConfigurationSource(@Value("${app.cors.origins}") String origins) {
        CorsConfiguration configuration = new CorsConfiguration();
        configuration.setAllowedOrigins(Arrays.stream(origins.split(",")).map(String::trim).toList());
        configuration.setAllowedMethods(Arrays.asList("GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"));
        configuration.setAllowedHeaders(Arrays.asList("Authorization", "Content-Type", "Idempotency-Key"));
        configuration.setExposedHeaders(Arrays.asList("Location"));
        UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource source = new UrlBasedCorsConfigurationSource();
        source.registerCorsConfiguration("/**", configuration);
        return source;
    }

    @Bean
    PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder() {
        return new BCryptPasswordEncoder();
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/config/SecurityConfig.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.domain;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.data.annotation.Id;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.index.CompoundIndex;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.mapping.Document;

@Document("bookings")
@CompoundIndex(name = "user_idempotency_unique", def = "{'userId': 1, 'idempotencyKey': 1}", unique = true)
@CompoundIndex(name = "booking_expiry_idx", def = "{'status': 1, 'expiresAt': 1}")
public record Booking(
        @Id String id,
        String pnr,
        String userId,
        String flightId,
        String seatNumber,
        String passengerName,
        String passengerEmail,
        BigDecimal amount,
        String currency,
        BookingStatus status,
        String razorpayOrderId,
        String razorpayPaymentId,
        Instant createdAt,
        String idempotencyKey,
        TripDetails tripDetails,
        Instant expiresAt) {

    public Booking(String id, String pnr, String userId, String flightId, String seatNumber,
                   String passengerName, String passengerEmail, BigDecimal amount, String currency,
                   BookingStatus status, String razorpayOrderId, String razorpayPaymentId,
                   Instant createdAt, String idempotencyKey) {
        this(id, pnr, userId, flightId, seatNumber, passengerName, passengerEmail, amount,
                currency, status, razorpayOrderId, razorpayPaymentId, createdAt, idempotencyKey, null, null);
    }

    public Booking(String id, String pnr, String userId, String flightId, String seatNumber,
                   String passengerName, String passengerEmail, BigDecimal amount, String currency,
                   BookingStatus status, String razorpayOrderId, String razorpayPaymentId,
                   Instant createdAt, String idempotencyKey, TripDetails tripDetails) {
        this(id, pnr, userId, flightId, seatNumber, passengerName, passengerEmail, amount,
                currency, status, razorpayOrderId, razorpayPaymentId, createdAt, idempotencyKey,
                tripDetails, null);
    }

    public Booking withPaymentOrder(String orderId) {
        return new Booking(id, pnr, userId, flightId, seatNumber, passengerName,
                passengerEmail, amount, currency, status, orderId, razorpayPaymentId,
                createdAt, idempotencyKey, tripDetails, expiresAt);
    }

    public Booking confirmed(String paymentId) {
        return new Booking(id, pnr, userId, flightId, seatNumber, passengerName,
                passengerEmail, amount, currency, BookingStatus.CONFIRMED, razorpayOrderId,
                paymentId, createdAt, idempotencyKey, tripDetails, null);
    }

    public Booking cancelled() {
        return new Booking(id, pnr, userId, flightId, seatNumber, passengerName,
                passengerEmail, amount, currency, BookingStatus.CANCELLED, razorpayOrderId,
                razorpayPaymentId, createdAt, idempotencyKey, tripDetails, expiresAt);
    }

    public Booking expired() {
        return new Booking(id, pnr, userId, flightId, seatNumber, passengerName,
                passengerEmail, amount, currency, BookingStatus.EXPIRED, razorpayOrderId,
                razorpayPaymentId, createdAt, idempotencyKey, tripDetails, expiresAt);
    }

    public record TripDetails(String returnFlightId, List<String> seatNumbers,
                              List<String> returnSeatNumbers, String travelClass,
                              List<Passenger> passengers) {
        public TripDetails {
            seatNumbers = seatNumbers == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(seatNumbers);
            returnSeatNumbers = returnSeatNumbers == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(returnSeatNumbers);
            passengers = passengers == null ? List.of() : List.copyOf(passengers);
        }
    }

    public record Passenger(String name, String nationality, LocalDate passportExpiryDate) {
    }
}

package com.jetsetgo.domain;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

import org.springframework.data.annotation.Id;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.index.CompoundIndex;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.mapping.Document;

@Document("flights")
@CompoundIndex(name = "flight_route_departure_idx",
        def = "{'originCode': 1, 'destinationCode': 1, 'departure': 1}")
@CompoundIndex(name = "flight_template_departure_idx", def = "{'routeTemplateId': 1, 'departure': 1}")
public record Flight(
        @Id String id,
        String airline,
        String flightNumber,
        String originCode,
        String originCity,
        String destinationCode,
        String destinationCity,
        Instant departure,
        Instant arrival,
        BigDecimal price,
        String currency,
        int capacity,
        String status,
        String dataSource,
        String originTimeZone,
        String destinationTimeZone,
        String aircraft,
        String originTerminal,
        String destinationTerminal,
        String routeTemplateId,
        Boolean templateDisabled) {

    public Flight(String id, String airline, String flightNumber, String originCode,
                  String originCity, String destinationCode, String destinationCity,
                  Instant departure, Instant arrival, BigDecimal price, String currency,
                  int capacity, String status, String dataSource) {
        this(id, airline, flightNumber, originCode, originCity, destinationCode, destinationCity,
                departure, arrival, price, currency, capacity, status, dataSource, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, false);
    }

    public Flight(String id, String airline, String flightNumber, String originCode,
                  String originCity, String destinationCode, String destinationCity,
                  Instant departure, Instant arrival, BigDecimal price, String currency,
                  int capacity, String status, String dataSource, String originTimeZone,
                  String destinationTimeZone) {
        this(id, airline, flightNumber, originCode, originCity, destinationCode, destinationCity,
                departure, arrival, price, currency, capacity, status, dataSource, originTimeZone,
                destinationTimeZone, null, null, null, null, false);
    }
}

package com.jetsetgo.repository;

import org.springframework.data.mongodb.repository.Query;

public interface FlightRepository extends MongoRepository<Flight, String> {
    List<Flight> findByOriginCodeIgnoreCaseAndDestinationCodeIgnoreCase(String origin, String destination);

    @Query(value = "{ 'departure': { $gt: ?0 }, 'status': { $in: ?1 }, 'templateDisabled': { $ne: true } }",
            sort = "{ 'departure': 1 }")
    List<Flight> findUpcomingForBrowsing(Instant after, List<String> statuses, Pageable pageable);

    @Query(value = "{ 'dataSource': 'DEMO', 'status': 'SCHEDULED', 'departure': { $gt: ?0 }, "
            + "'templateDisabled': { $ne: true } }", sort = "{ 'departure': 1 }")
    List<Flight> findFeaturedDemoFlights(Instant after, Pageable pageable);
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AdminService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.UUID;

import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.AppUser;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.SeatInventory;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.FlightRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.UserRepository;

@Service
public class AdminService {
    private static final List<String> FLIGHT_STATUSES =
            List.of("SCHEDULED", "DELAYED", "CANCELLED", "DEPARTED", "COMPLETED");
    private final FlightRepository flights;
    private final BookingRepository bookings;
    private final UserRepository users;
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;
    private final BookingService bookingService;

    public AdminService(FlightRepository flights, BookingRepository bookings,
                        UserRepository users, MongoTemplate mongo,
                        BookingService bookingService) {
        this.flights = flights;
        this.bookings = bookings;
        this.users = users;
        this.mongo = mongo;
        this.bookingService = bookingService;
    }

    public Summary summary() {
        List<Booking> allBookings = bookings.findAll();
        BigDecimal revenue = allBookings.stream()
                .filter(booking -> booking.status() == BookingStatus.CONFIRMED)
                .map(Booking::amount).reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
        long cancelled = allBookings.stream()
                .filter(booking -> booking.status() == BookingStatus.CANCELLED).count();
        List<Flight> allFlights = flights.findAll();
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        long activeFlights = allFlights.stream().filter(flight ->
                List.of("SCHEDULED", "DELAYED").contains(flight.status())
                        && flight.departure().isAfter(now)).count();
        long upcomingDepartures = allFlights.stream().filter(flight ->
                flight.departure().isAfter(now) && flight.departure().isBefore(now.plusSeconds(86400))
                        && List.of("SCHEDULED", "DELAYED").contains(flight.status())).count();
        long confirmed = allBookings.stream().filter(booking ->
                booking.status() == BookingStatus.CONFIRMED).count();
        long reserved = allBookings.stream().filter(booking ->
                List.of(BookingStatus.CONFIRMED, BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT,
                        BookingStatus.PAYMENT_PROCESSING).contains(booking.status()))
                .mapToLong(AdminService::seatCount).sum();
        long capacity = allFlights.stream().filter(flight -> flight.departure().isAfter(now))
                .mapToLong(Flight::capacity).sum();
        double occupancy = capacity == 0 ? 0 : Math.min(100.0, reserved * 100.0 / capacity);
        return new Summary(flights.count(), activeFlights, upcomingDepartures, allBookings.size(),
                confirmed, users.count(), revenue, cancelled, occupancy);
    }

    public List<Flight> flights(String origin, String destination, LocalDate date, String flightNumber) {
        return flights.findAll().stream()
                .filter(flight -> origin == null || origin.isBlank()
                        || flight.originCode().equalsIgnoreCase(origin.trim()))
                .filter(flight -> destination == null || destination.isBlank()
                        || flight.destinationCode().equalsIgnoreCase(destination.trim()))
                .filter(flight -> flightNumber == null || flightNumber.isBlank()
                        || flight.flightNumber().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
                        .contains(flightNumber.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)))
                .filter(flight -> date == null || isOnLocalDate(flight, date))
                .sorted(java.util.Comparator.comparing(Flight::departure)).toList();
    }

    public Flight addFlight(NewFlight request) {
        if (request.originCode().equalsIgnoreCase(request.destinationCode())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Origin and destination airports must be different");
        }
        if (!request.departure().isAfter(Instant.now()) || !request.arrival().isAfter(request.departure())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Flight arrival and departure times are invalid");
        }
        Flight flight = flights.save(new Flight(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                request.airline().trim(), request.flightNumber().trim().toUpperCase(),
                request.originCode().trim().toUpperCase(), request.originCity().trim(),
                request.destinationCode().trim().toUpperCase(), request.destinationCity().trim(),
                request.departure(), request.arrival(), request.price(), "INR",
                request.capacity(), "SCHEDULED", "DEMO", request.originTimeZone(),
                request.destinationTimeZone(), request.aircraft(), request.originTerminal(),
                request.destinationTerminal(), null, false));
        return flight;
    }

    public Flight updateFlight(String flightId, NewFlight request) {
        Flight existing = flights.findById(flightId).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Flight not found"));
        if (!existing.departure().isAfter(Instant.now()) || existing.routeTemplateId() != null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Edit the recurring route template instead, or preserve this historical flight");
        }
        if (bookings.existsByFlightId(flightId)
                || mongo.exists(Query.query(Criteria.where("tripDetails.returnFlightId").is(flightId)), Booking.class)
                || mongo.exists(Query.query(Criteria.where("flightId").is(flightId)
                        .and("available").is(false)), SeatInventory.class)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "A flight with booking history cannot be edited");
        }
        validateTimes(request.departure(), request.arrival());
        if (request.originCode().equalsIgnoreCase(request.destinationCode())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Origin and destination airports must be different");
        }
        mongo.remove(Query.query(Criteria.where("flightId").is(flightId)), SeatInventory.class);
        Flight updated = new Flight(existing.id(), request.airline().trim(),
                request.flightNumber().trim().toUpperCase(), request.originCode().trim().toUpperCase(),
                request.originCity().trim(), request.destinationCode().trim().toUpperCase(),
                request.destinationCity().trim(), request.departure(), request.arrival(), request.price(),
                "INR", request.capacity(), existing.status(), "DEMO", request.originTimeZone(),
                request.destinationTimeZone(), request.aircraft(), request.originTerminal(),
                request.destinationTerminal(), null, false);
        return flights.save(updated);
    }

    public Flight changeStatus(String flightId, String status) {
        String normalized = status == null ? "" : status.toUpperCase();
        if (!FLIGHT_STATUSES.contains(normalized)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Unsupported flight status");
        }
        Flight flight = flights.findById(flightId).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Flight not found"));
        boolean validTransition = switch (flight.status()) {
            case "SCHEDULED" -> List.of("DELAYED", "CANCELLED", "DEPARTED").contains(normalized);
            case "DELAYED" -> List.of("SCHEDULED", "CANCELLED", "DEPARTED").contains(normalized);
            case "DEPARTED" -> "COMPLETED".equals(normalized);
            default -> false;
        };
        if (!validTransition) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Flight status cannot transition from " + flight.status() + " to " + normalized);
        }
        if (("DEPARTED".equals(normalized) && flight.departure().isAfter(Instant.now()))
                || ("COMPLETED".equals(normalized) && flight.arrival().isAfter(Instant.now()))) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "A flight cannot be marked departed or completed before its scheduled time");
        }
        Flight updated = new Flight(flight.id(), flight.airline(), flight.flightNumber(),
                flight.originCode(), flight.originCity(), flight.destinationCode(),
                flight.destinationCity(), flight.departure(), flight.arrival(), flight.price(),
                flight.currency(), flight.capacity(), normalized, flight.dataSource(),
                flight.originTimeZone(), flight.destinationTimeZone(), flight.aircraft(),
                flight.originTerminal(), flight.destinationTerminal(), flight.routeTemplateId(),
                flight.templateDisabled());
        return flights.save(updated);
    }

    public void deleteFlight(String flightId) {
        Flight flight = flights.findById(flightId).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Flight not found"));
        if (flight.routeTemplateId() != null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Disable the route template instead of deleting a generated flight");
        }
        if (bookings.existsByFlightId(flightId)
                || mongo.exists(org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query.query(
                        org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria.where("tripDetails.returnFlightId")
                                .is(flightId)), Booking.class)
                || mongo.exists(Query.query(Criteria.where("flightId").is(flightId)
                        .and("available").is(false)), SeatInventory.class)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "A flight with booking history cannot be deleted");
        }
        flights.deleteById(flightId);
        mongo.remove(org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query.query(
                org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria.where("flightId").is(flightId)),
                SeatInventory.class);
    }

    public List<Booking> bookings() {
        return bookings.findAll().stream()
                .sorted(java.util.Comparator.comparing(Booking::createdAt).reversed()).toList();
    }

    public List<Booking> bookings(String status) {
        return bookings.findAll().stream()
                .filter(booking -> status == null || status.isBlank()
                        || booking.status().name().equalsIgnoreCase(status.trim()))
                .sorted(java.util.Comparator.comparing(Booking::createdAt).reversed()).toList();
    }

    public Booking cancelBooking(String bookingId) {
        Booking booking = bookings.findById(bookingId).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Booking not found"));
        return bookingService.cancel(booking.userId(), bookingId);
    }

    public List<UserSummary> users() {
        return users.findAll().stream().map(user ->
                new UserSummary(user.id(), user.name(), user.email(), user.role())).toList();
    }

    private static long seatCount(Booking booking) {
        if (booking.tripDetails() == null || booking.tripDetails().seatNumbers().isEmpty()) {
            return 1;
        }
        return booking.tripDetails().seatNumbers().size() + booking.tripDetails().returnSeatNumbers().size();
    }

    private static boolean isOnLocalDate(Flight flight, LocalDate date) {
        try {
            ZoneId zone = flight.originTimeZone() == null || flight.originTimeZone().isBlank()
                    ? ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata") : ZoneId.of(flight.originTimeZone());
            return flight.departure().atZone(zone).toLocalDate().equals(date);
        } catch (java.time.DateTimeException invalidZone) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static void validateTimes(Instant departure, Instant arrival) {
        if (departure == null || arrival == null || !departure.isAfter(Instant.now())
                || !arrival.isAfter(departure)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Flight arrival and departure times are invalid");
        }
    }

    public record Summary(long totalFlights, long activeFlights, long upcomingDepartures,
                          long totalBookings, long confirmedBookings, long registeredUsers,
                          BigDecimal confirmedRevenue, long cancelledBookings, double seatOccupancy) {}
    public record NewFlight(String airline, String flightNumber, String originCode,
                            String originCity, String destinationCode, String destinationCity,
                            Instant departure, Instant arrival, BigDecimal price, int capacity,
                            String originTimeZone, String destinationTimeZone, String aircraft,
                            String originTerminal, String destinationTerminal) {}
    public record UserSummary(String id, String name, String email, AppUser.Role role) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AdminService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AirportDatasetImporter.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.Reader;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVFormat;
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVParser;
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVRecord;
import org.bson.Document;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.BulkOperations;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;

@Service
public class AirportDatasetImporter {
    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AirportDatasetImporter.class);
    private static final String DATASET_URL =
            "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/davidmegginson/ourairports-data/main/";
    private static final String IMPORTS_COLLECTION = "airport_dataset_imports";
    private static final String IMPORT_ID = "ourairports-iata-v2";
    private static final Pattern IATA_CODE = Pattern.compile("[A-Z]{3}");
    private static final Set<String> AIRPORT_TYPES =
            Set.of("large_airport", "medium_airport", "small_airport");
    private static final int BATCH_SIZE = 750;

    private final MongoTemplate mongo;
    private final HttpClient http = HttpClient.newBuilder()
            .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(20))
            .followRedirects(HttpClient.Redirect.NORMAL)
            .build();

    public AirportDatasetImporter(MongoTemplate mongo) {
        this.mongo = mongo;
    }

    public int importIfNeeded() {
        Query markerQuery = Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(IMPORT_ID));
        if (mongo.exists(markerQuery, IMPORTS_COLLECTION)) {
            log.info("OurAirports catalog was already imported; skipping the one-time import.");
            return 0;
        }

        try {
            Map<String, String> countries = loadNames("countries.csv", "code", "name");
            Map<String, String> regions = loadNames("regions.csv", "code", "name");
            Map<String, Airport> airports = loadAirports(countries, regions);
            if (airports.isEmpty()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("OurAirports returned no valid IATA airport records.");
            }

            List<Airport> batch = new ArrayList<>(BATCH_SIZE);
            for (Airport airport : airports.values()) {
                batch.add(airport);
                if (batch.size() == BATCH_SIZE) {
                    upsert(batch);
                    batch.clear();
                }
            }
            if (!batch.isEmpty()) {
                upsert(batch);
            }

            mongo.insert(new Document("_id", IMPORT_ID)
                    .append("source", DATASET_URL)
                    .append("airportCount", airports.size())
                    .append("importedAt", Instant.now()), IMPORTS_COLLECTION);
            log.info("Imported {} unique IATA airports from OurAirports.", airports.size());
            return airports.size();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Could not read the OurAirports CSV dataset.", e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new IllegalStateException("OurAirports import was interrupted.", e);
        }
    }

    private Map<String, String> loadNames(String filename, String codeField, String nameField)
            throws IOException, InterruptedException {
        Map<String, String> names = new HashMap<>();
        try (CSVParser parser = parse(filename)) {
            for (CSVRecord record : parser) {
                String code = value(record, codeField).toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
                String name = value(record, nameField);
                if (!code.isBlank() && !name.isBlank()) {
                    names.put(code, name);
                }
            }
        }
        return names;
    }

    private Map<String, Airport> loadAirports(Map<String, String> countries, Map<String, String> regions)
            throws IOException, InterruptedException {
        try (CSVParser parser = parse("airports.csv")) {
            return mapAirportRecords(parser, countries, regions);
        }
    }

    static Map<String, Airport> mapAirportRecords(Iterable<CSVRecord> records,
                                                   Map<String, String> countries,
                                                   Map<String, String> regions) {
        Map<String, Airport> unique = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (CSVRecord record : records) {
            String type = value(record, "type");
            String code = value(record, "iata_code").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
            String city = value(record, "municipality");
            String name = value(record, "name");
            String countryCode = value(record, "iso_country").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
            if (!AIRPORT_TYPES.contains(type) || !IATA_CODE.matcher(code).matches()
                    || city.isBlank() || name.isBlank() || countryCode.isBlank()) {
                continue;
            }

            String regionCode = value(record, "iso_region").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
            String regionName = regions.getOrDefault(regionCode, localRegion(regionCode, countryCode));
            Airport airport = new Airport(code, city, name, regionName,
                    countries.getOrDefault(countryCode, countryCode), countryCode, null,
                    "yes".equalsIgnoreCase(value(record, "scheduled_service")), type,
                    decimal(record, "latitude_deg"), decimal(record, "longitude_deg"),
                    "OURAIRPORTS");
            unique.merge(code, airport, (first, next) -> quality(next) > quality(first) ? next : first);
        }
        return unique;
    }

    private CSVParser parse(String filename) throws IOException, InterruptedException {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(URI.create(DATASET_URL + filename))
                .timeout(Duration.ofMinutes(2))
                .header("User-Agent", "JetSetGo-AirportCatalog/1.0")
                .GET()
                .build();
        HttpResponse<InputStream> response = http.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofInputStream());
        if (response.statusCode() != 200) {
            response.body().close();
            throw new IOException("OurAirports request for " + filename + " returned HTTP "
                    + response.statusCode() + ".");
        }
        Reader reader = new InputStreamReader(response.body(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        return CSVFormat.DEFAULT.builder()
                .setHeader()
                .setSkipHeaderRecord(true)
                .build()
                .parse(reader);
    }

    private void upsert(List<Airport> airports) {
        BulkOperations bulk = mongo.bulkOps(BulkOperations.BulkMode.UNORDERED, Airport.class);
        for (Airport airport : airports) {
            Update update = new Update()
                    .set("city", airport.city())
                    .set("name", airport.name())
                    .set("stateOrRegion", airport.stateOrRegion())
                    .set("country", airport.country())
                    .set("countryCode", airport.countryCode())
                    .set("timeZone", airport.timeZone())
                    .set("scheduledService", airport.scheduledService())
                    .set("airportType", airport.airportType())
                    .set("latitude", airport.latitude())
                    .set("longitude", airport.longitude())
                    .set("dataSource", airport.dataSource());
            bulk.upsert(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(airport.code())), update);
        }
        bulk.execute();
    }

    private static int quality(Airport airport) {
        int typeQuality = switch (airport.airportType()) {
            case "large_airport" -> 3;
            case "medium_airport" -> 2;
            default -> 1;
        };
        return (Boolean.TRUE.equals(airport.scheduledService()) ? 10 : 0) + typeQuality;
    }

    private static String localRegion(String regionCode, String countryCode) {
        String prefix = countryCode + "-";
        return regionCode.startsWith(prefix) ? regionCode.substring(prefix.length()) : regionCode;
    }

    private static String value(CSVRecord record, String field) {
        String value = record.get(field);
        return value == null ? "" : value.trim();
    }

    private static Double decimal(CSVRecord record, String field) {
        String value = value(record, field);
        if (value.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return Double.valueOf(value);
        } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AirportDatasetImporter.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AirportService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;

@Service
public class AirportService {
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;

    public AirportService(MongoTemplate mongo) {
        this.mongo = mongo;
    }

    public List<Airport> search(String search, String countryCode, boolean scheduledOnly) {
        Query query = new Query();
        List<Criteria> filters = new ArrayList<>();
        if (search != null && !search.isBlank()) {
            for (String token : search.trim().split("\\s+")) {
                String safePattern = Pattern.quote(token);
                filters.add(new Criteria().orOperator(
                        Criteria.where("_id").regex(safePattern, "i"),
                        Criteria.where("city").regex(safePattern, "i"),
                        Criteria.where("name").regex(safePattern, "i"),
                        Criteria.where("stateOrRegion").regex(safePattern, "i"),
                        Criteria.where("country").regex(safePattern, "i"),
                        Criteria.where("countryCode").regex(safePattern, "i")));
            }
        }
        if (countryCode != null && !countryCode.isBlank()) {
            filters.add(Criteria.where("countryCode").is(countryCode.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)));
        }
        if (scheduledOnly) {
            filters.add(Criteria.where("scheduledService").is(true));
        }
        if (!filters.isEmpty()) {
            query.addCriteria(filters.size() == 1
                    ? filters.get(0)
                    : new Criteria().andOperator(filters.toArray(Criteria[]::new)));
        }
        return mongo.find(query.with(Sort.by(
                Sort.Order.desc("scheduledService"),
                Sort.Order.asc("airportType"),
                Sort.Order.asc("city"),
                Sort.Order.asc("_id"))).limit(30), Airport.class);
    }

    public List<String> countryCodes() {
        List<String> codes = mongo.getCollection("airports")
                .distinct("countryCode", String.class)
                .into(new ArrayList<>());
        return codes.stream()
                .filter(code -> code != null && !code.isBlank())
                .map(code -> code.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT))
                .distinct()
                .sorted()
                .toList();
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AirportService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AuthService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.config.JwtService;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.AppUser;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.UserRepository;

@Service
public class AuthService {
    private final UserRepository users;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwords;
    private final JwtService tokens;

    public AuthService(UserRepository users, PasswordEncoder passwords, JwtService tokens) {
        this.users = users;
        this.passwords = passwords;
        this.tokens = tokens;
    }

    public AuthResult register(String name, String email, String password) {
        String normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (users.existsByEmailIgnoreCase(normalizedEmail)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "An account with this email already exists");
        }
        AppUser user = users.save(new AppUser(null, normalizedEmail,
                passwords.encode(password), name.trim(), AppUser.Role.CUSTOMER));
        return result(user);
    }

    public AuthResult login(String email, String password) {
        AppUser user = users.findByEmailIgnoreCase(email.trim())
                .filter(account -> passwords.matches(password, account.passwordHash()))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED,
                        "Email or password is incorrect. Check your details and try again."));
        return result(user);
    }

    private AuthResult result(AppUser user) {
        return new AuthResult(tokens.issue(user), new UserView(user.id(), user.name(), user.email(), user.role()));
    }

    public record AuthResult(String token, UserView user) {}
    public record UserView(String id, String name, String email, AppUser.Role role) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AuthService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/BookingExpiryService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;

@Service
public class BookingExpiryService {
    private final BookingRepository bookings;
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;
    private final SeatInventoryService seats;

    public BookingExpiryService(BookingRepository bookings, MongoTemplate mongo, SeatInventoryService seats) {
        this.bookings = bookings;
        this.mongo = mongo;
        this.seats = seats;
    }

    @Scheduled(fixedDelayString = "${app.booking-expiry-scan-ms:60000}")
    public void releaseExpiredReservations() {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        List<Booking> expired = bookings.findByStatusAndExpiresAtBefore(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, now);
        for (Booking booking : expired) {
            Booking transitioned = mongo.findAndModify(
                    Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(booking.id())
                            .and("status").is(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT)
                            .and("expiresAt").lt(now)),
                    Update.update("status", BookingStatus.EXPIRED),
                    FindAndModifyOptions.options().returnNew(true), Booking.class);
            if (transitioned == null) {
                continue;
            }
            List<String> outbound = booking.tripDetails() == null
                    || booking.tripDetails().seatNumbers().isEmpty()
                    ? List.of(booking.seatNumber()) : booking.tripDetails().seatNumbers();
            outbound.forEach(seat -> seats.release(booking.flightId(), seat, booking.id()));
            if (booking.tripDetails() != null && booking.tripDetails().returnFlightId() != null) {
                booking.tripDetails().returnSeatNumbers().forEach(seat ->
                        seats.release(booking.tripDetails().returnFlightId(), seat, booking.id()));
            }
        }
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/BookingExpiryService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/BookingService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking.Passenger;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking.TripDetails;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.AirportRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.FlightRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.service.fare.FarePolicyResolver;

@Service
public class BookingService {
    private static final int MAX_PASSENGERS = 6;
    private static final Pattern SEAT_NUMBER = Pattern.compile("[1-9][0-9]?[A-F]");
    private final BookingRepository bookings;
    private final FlightRepository flights;
    private final AirportRepository airports;
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;
    private final PaymentProcessor payments;
    private final SeatInventoryService seatInventory;
    private final FarePolicyResolver farePolicies;

    public BookingService(BookingRepository bookings, FlightRepository flights, AirportRepository airports,
                         MongoTemplate mongo, PaymentProcessor payments,
                         SeatInventoryService seatInventory, FarePolicyResolver farePolicies) {
        this.bookings = bookings;
        this.flights = flights;
        this.airports = airports;
        this.mongo = mongo;
        this.payments = payments;
        this.seatInventory = seatInventory;
        this.farePolicies = farePolicies;
    }

    public Booking create(String userId, String idempotencyKey, CreateBooking request) {
        return bookings.findByUserIdAndIdempotencyKey(userId, idempotencyKey)
                .orElseGet(() -> reserveAndCreate(userId, idempotencyKey, request));
    }

    private Booking reserveAndCreate(String userId, String idempotencyKey, CreateBooking request) {
        Flight flight = flights.findById(request.flightId()).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Flight not found"));
        ensureBookable(flight);
        Flight returnFlight = null;
        if (request.returnFlightId() != null && !request.returnFlightId().isBlank()) {
            if (request.returnFlightId().equals(flight.id())) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Return flight must be different");
            }
            returnFlight = flights.findById(request.returnFlightId()).orElseThrow(() ->
                    new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Return flight not found"));
            ensureBookable(returnFlight);
            if (!flight.originCode().equalsIgnoreCase(returnFlight.destinationCode())
                    || !flight.destinationCode().equalsIgnoreCase(returnFlight.originCode())
                    || !returnFlight.departure().isAfter(flight.arrival())) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                        "Return flight must be a later scheduled flight on the reverse route");
            }
        } else if (request.returnSeatNumbers() != null && !request.returnSeatNumbers().isEmpty()) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Return seats require a return flight");
        }

        List<PassengerInput> requestedPassengers = request.passengers();
        if (requestedPassengers == null || requestedPassengers.isEmpty()) {
            requestedPassengers = List.of(new PassengerInput(request.passengerName(), null, null));
        }
        if (requestedPassengers.size() > MAX_PASSENGERS) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "A booking can include at most six passengers");
        }
        List<Passenger> passengers = requestedPassengers.stream().map(this::normalizePassenger).toList();
        List<String> seatNumbers = normalizeSeats(request.seatNumbers(), request.seatNumber(),
                passengers.size(), "outbound");
        List<String> returnSeatNumbers = returnFlight == null ? List.of()
                : normalizeSeats(request.returnSeatNumbers(), null, passengers.size(), "return");
        String travelClass = normalizeTravelClass(request.travelClass());
        boolean international = isInternational(flight);
        if (international) {
            validateTravelDocuments(passengers);
        }
        if (returnFlight != null && isInternational(returnFlight) != international) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Outbound and return flights must use the same domestic or international journey type");
        }
        if (returnFlight != null && !flight.currency().equals(returnFlight.currency())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Outbound and return flights must use the same currency");
        }

        String bookingId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        List<ReservedSeat> reservedSeats = new ArrayList<>();
        try {
            seatInventory.ensureInitialized(flight);
            reserveSeats(flight.id(), seatNumbers, travelClass, bookingId, reservedSeats);
            if (returnFlight != null) {
                seatInventory.ensureInitialized(returnFlight);
                reserveSeats(returnFlight.id(), returnSeatNumbers, travelClass, bookingId, reservedSeats);
            }
        } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
            releaseSeats(reservedSeats, bookingId);
            throw failure;
        }

        BigDecimal baseFare = returnFlight == null ? flight.price()
                : flight.price().add(returnFlight.price());
        BigDecimal amount = farePolicies.calculate(travelClass, baseFare, passengers.size());
        TripDetails tripDetails = new TripDetails(returnFlight == null ? null : returnFlight.id(),
                seatNumbers, returnSeatNumbers, travelClass, passengers);
        Instant createdAt = Instant.now();
        Booking booking = new Booking(bookingId, UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(),
                userId, flight.id(), seatNumbers.getFirst(), passengers.getFirst().name(),
                request.passengerEmail().trim().toLowerCase(), amount, flight.currency(),
                BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, null, null, createdAt, idempotencyKey, tripDetails,
                createdAt.plusSeconds(15 * 60L));
        try {
            return bookings.save(booking);
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException duplicate) {
            releaseSeats(reservedSeats, bookingId);
            return bookings.findByUserIdAndIdempotencyKey(userId, idempotencyKey)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> duplicate);
        } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
            releaseSeats(reservedSeats, bookingId);
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    public List<Booking> forUser(String userId) {
        return bookings.findByUserIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(userId);
    }

    public Booking getForUser(String userId, String bookingId) {
        return bookings.findById(bookingId)
                .filter(booking -> booking.userId().equals(userId))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Booking not found"));
    }

    public Booking cancel(String userId, String bookingId) {
        Booking booking = getForUser(userId, bookingId);
        if (booking.status() == BookingStatus.CANCELLED) return booking;
        Flight flight = flights.findById(booking.flightId()).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Flight not found"));
        Flight returnFlight = booking.tripDetails() == null || booking.tripDetails().returnFlightId() == null
                ? null : flights.findById(booking.tripDetails().returnFlightId()).orElseThrow(() ->
                        new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Return flight not found"));
        if (!flight.departure().isAfter(Instant.now())
                || returnFlight != null && !returnFlight.departure().isAfter(Instant.now())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "A departed flight cannot be cancelled");
        }
        BookingStatus originalStatus = booking.status();
        if (originalStatus != BookingStatus.CONFIRMED && originalStatus != BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This booking is already being cancelled");
        }
        Booking cancelling = mongo.findAndModify(
                Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(booking.id()).and("status").is(originalStatus)),
                Update.update("status", BookingStatus.CANCELLING),
                FindAndModifyOptions.options().returnNew(true), Booking.class);
        if (cancelling == null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This booking has already changed");
        }
        if (originalStatus == BookingStatus.CONFIRMED) {
            try {
                payments.refund(booking);
            } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
                mongo.updateFirst(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(booking.id())
                                .and("status").is(BookingStatus.CANCELLING)),
                        Update.update("status", originalStatus), Booking.class);
                throw failure;
            }
        }
        Booking cancelled = bookings.save(cancelling.cancelled());
        List<String> outboundSeats = booking.tripDetails() == null || booking.tripDetails().seatNumbers().isEmpty()
                ? List.of(booking.seatNumber()) : booking.tripDetails().seatNumbers();
        outboundSeats.forEach(seat -> seatInventory.release(booking.flightId(), seat, booking.id()));
        if (returnFlight != null) {
            booking.tripDetails().returnSeatNumbers().forEach(seat ->
                    seatInventory.release(returnFlight.id(), seat, booking.id()));
        }
        return cancelled;
    }

    private void ensureBookable(Flight flight) {
        if (!"SCHEDULED".equals(flight.status()) || Boolean.TRUE.equals(flight.templateDisabled())
                || !flight.departure().isAfter(Instant.now())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This flight is not available for booking");
        }
    }

    private void reserveSeats(String flightId, List<String> seats, String travelClass,
                              String reservationId, List<ReservedSeat> reservedSeats) {
        for (String seat : seats) {
            seatInventory.reserve(flightId, seat, travelClass, reservationId);
            reservedSeats.add(new ReservedSeat(flightId, seat));
        }
    }

    private List<String> normalizeSeats(List<String> requested, String primarySeat,
                                        int passengerCount, String leg) {
        List<String> seats = requested == null || requested.isEmpty()
                ? primarySeat == null ? List.of() : List.of(primarySeat)
                : requested.stream().map(seat -> seat == null ? "" : seat.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)).toList();
        if (seats.size() != passengerCount || seats.stream().anyMatch(seat -> !SEAT_NUMBER.matcher(seat).matches())
                || seats.stream().distinct().count() != seats.size()
                || primarySeat != null && !seats.getFirst().equalsIgnoreCase(primarySeat.trim())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Choose one unique " + leg + " seat per passenger");
        }
        return seats;
    }

    private Passenger normalizePassenger(PassengerInput passenger) {
        if (passenger == null || passenger.name() == null || passenger.name().isBlank()
                || passenger.name().trim().length() > 100) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Passenger names are required");
        }
        String nationality = passenger.nationality() == null ? ""
                : passenger.nationality().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!nationality.isBlank() && !nationality.matches("[A-Z]{2}")) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Nationality must be a two-letter country code");
        }
        return new Passenger(passenger.name().trim(), nationality.isBlank() ? null : nationality,
                passenger.passportExpiryDate());
    }

    private String normalizeTravelClass(String travelClass) {
        String normalized = travelClass == null || travelClass.isBlank()
                ? "ECONOMY" : travelClass.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        farePolicies.resolve(normalized);
        return normalized;
    }

    private boolean isInternational(Flight flight) {
        Airport origin = airports.findById(flight.originCode()).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Airport catalog data is required to book a flight"));
        Airport destination = airports.findById(flight.destinationCode()).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Airport catalog data is required to book a flight"));
        if (origin.countryCode() == null || origin.countryCode().isBlank()
                || destination.countryCode() == null || destination.countryCode().isBlank()) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Airport country data is required to book a flight");
        }
        return !origin.countryCode().equalsIgnoreCase(destination.countryCode());
    }

    private void validateTravelDocuments(List<Passenger> passengers) {
        for (Passenger passenger : passengers) {
            if (passenger.nationality() == null || passenger.passportExpiryDate() == null
                    || !passenger.passportExpiryDate().isAfter(LocalDate.now())) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                        "International demo bookings require each passenger's nationality and a future passport expiry date");
            }
        }
    }

    private void releaseSeats(List<ReservedSeat> seats, String reservationId) {
        seats.forEach(seat -> seatInventory.release(seat.flightId(), seat.seatNumber(), reservationId));
    }

    private record ReservedSeat(String flightId, String seatNumber) {}

    public record CreateBooking(String flightId, String seatNumber,
                                String passengerName, String passengerEmail, String returnFlightId,
                                List<String> seatNumbers, List<String> returnSeatNumbers,
                                List<PassengerInput> passengers, String travelClass) {}
    public record PassengerInput(String name, String nationality, LocalDate passportExpiryDate) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/BookingService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/DemoPaymentService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.DemoPayment;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.DemoPaymentRepository;

@Service
public class DemoPaymentService {
    private final BookingRepository bookings;
    private final DemoPaymentRepository payments;
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;
    private final boolean demoMode;

    public DemoPaymentService(BookingRepository bookings, DemoPaymentRepository payments,
                              MongoTemplate mongo, @Value("${payment.mode:DEMO}") String paymentMode) {
        this.bookings = bookings;
        this.payments = payments;
        this.mongo = mongo;
        this.demoMode = "DEMO".equalsIgnoreCase(paymentMode);
    }

    public PaymentResult complete(String userId, String bookingId, String idempotencyKey,
                                  String method, boolean succeed) {
        requireDemoMode();
        DemoPayment previous = payments.findByUserIdAndIdempotencyKey(userId, idempotencyKey).orElse(null);
        if (previous != null) {
            if (!previous.bookingId().equals(bookingId)) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Payment retry key was already used");
            }
            Booking existingBooking = ownedBooking(userId, bookingId);
            if ("SUCCEEDED".equals(previous.status())
                    && existingBooking.status() == BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT) {
                existingBooking = confirmDemoBooking(existingBooking);
            } else if ("SUCCEEDED".equals(previous.status())
                    && existingBooking.status() != BookingStatus.CONFIRMED) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This booking payment is still processing");
            }
            return new PaymentResult(existingBooking, previous.reference(), previous.status());
        }

        Booking booking = ownedBooking(userId, bookingId);
        ensureNotExpired(booking);
        if (booking.status() != BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT) {
            if (booking.status() == BookingStatus.CONFIRMED && !succeed) {
                return new PaymentResult(booking, "already-confirmed", "SUCCEEDED");
            }
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This booking is not awaiting demo payment");
        }

        Booking processing = null;
        if (succeed) {
            processing = mongo.findAndModify(
                    Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(booking.id())
                            .and("status").is(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT)),
                    Update.update("status", BookingStatus.PAYMENT_PROCESSING),
                    FindAndModifyOptions.options().returnNew(true), Booking.class);
            if (processing == null) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This booking payment is already being processed");
            }
        }

        String status = succeed ? "SUCCEEDED" : "FAILED";
        String reference = "JSG-DEMO-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
        DemoPayment payment = new DemoPayment(null, booking.id(), userId, idempotencyKey, method,
                booking.amount(), booking.currency(), status, reference, Instant.now());
        try {
            payments.save(payment);
            Booking result = succeed ? bookings.save(processing.confirmed(null)) : booking;
            return new PaymentResult(result, reference, status);
        } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
            if (processing != null) {
                mongo.updateFirst(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(booking.id())
                                .and("status").is(BookingStatus.PAYMENT_PROCESSING)),
                        Update.update("status", BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT), Booking.class);
            }
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    public void refund(String bookingId) {
        requireDemoMode();
        DemoPayment payment = payments.findFirstByBookingIdAndStatusOrderByCreatedAtDesc(
                        bookingId, "SUCCEEDED")
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(
                        HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "The demo payment record was not found"));
        payments.save(payment.refunded());
    }

    private Booking confirmDemoBooking(Booking booking) {
        ensureNotExpired(booking);
        Booking processing = mongo.findAndModify(
                Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(booking.id())
                        .and("status").is(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT)),
                Update.update("status", BookingStatus.PAYMENT_PROCESSING),
                FindAndModifyOptions.options().returnNew(true), Booking.class);
        if (processing == null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This booking payment is already being processed");
        }
        return bookings.save(processing.confirmed(null));
    }

    private Booking ownedBooking(String userId, String bookingId) {
        return bookings.findById(bookingId)
                .filter(booking -> booking.userId().equals(userId))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Booking not found"));
    }

    private static void ensureNotExpired(Booking booking) {
        if (booking.expiresAt() != null && !booking.expiresAt().isAfter(Instant.now())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "This payment reservation has expired. Please create a new booking.");
        }
    }

    private void requireDemoMode() {
        if (!demoMode) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Demo payments are disabled; use Razorpay Test Mode.");
        }
    }

    public record PaymentResult(Booking booking, String reference, String status) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/DemoPaymentService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/FlightScheduleGenerator.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.util.Set;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.RouteTemplate;

public interface FlightScheduleGenerator {
    int generateRollingWindow(LocalDate firstDate, int days);

    List<Flight> ensureDemoRouteInstances(Airport origin, Airport destination, LocalDate date);

    Flight createInstance(RouteTemplate template, LocalDate departureDate);

    int updateFutureInstances(RouteTemplate template, Set<String> protectedFlightIds);

    int setTemplateEnabled(String templateId, boolean enabled, Set<String> protectedFlightIds);
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/FlightScheduleGenerator.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/FlightService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.DateTimeException;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.math.BigDecimal;

import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.AirportRepository;
