import com.jetsetgo.repository.FlightRepository;

@Service
public class FlightService {
    private static final List<String> SEARCHABLE_STATUSES = List.of("SCHEDULED", "DELAYED", "CANCELLED");
    private static final int SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT = 40;
    private final FlightRepository flights;
    private final AirportRepository airports;
    private final FlightScheduleGenerator schedules;

    public FlightService(FlightRepository flights, AirportRepository airports, FlightScheduleGenerator schedules) {
        this.flights = flights;
        this.airports = airports;
        this.schedules = schedules;
    }

    public List<Flight> search(String origin, String destination, LocalDate date, String sort,
                               String mode, String countryCode, String airline, BigDecimal maxPrice) {
        String normalizedMode = mode == null ? "" : mode.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!normalizedMode.isBlank() && !normalizedMode.equals("domestic")
                && !normalizedMode.equals("international")) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "mode must be domestic or international");
        }
        Airport originAirport = airport(origin);
        Airport destinationAirport = airport(destination);
        if ("domestic".equals(normalizedMode)) {
            if (countryCode == null || countryCode.isBlank()
                    || originAirport == null || destinationAirport == null
                    || !countryCode.equalsIgnoreCase(originAirport.countryCode())
                    || !countryCode.equalsIgnoreCase(destinationAirport.countryCode())) {
                return List.of();
            }
        }
        if ("international".equals(normalizedMode)) {
            if (originAirport == null || destinationAirport == null
                    || originAirport.countryCode() == null || destinationAirport.countryCode() == null
                    || originAirport.countryCode().isBlank() || destinationAirport.countryCode().isBlank()
                    || originAirport.countryCode().equalsIgnoreCase(destinationAirport.countryCode())) {
                return List.of();
            }
        }
        List<Flight> results;
        if (origin != null && !origin.isBlank() && destination != null && !destination.isBlank()) {
            List<Flight> routeFlights = new ArrayList<>(flights.findByOriginCodeIgnoreCaseAndDestinationCodeIgnoreCase(
                    origin.trim(), destination.trim()));
            ZoneId routeZone = timeZone(originAirport, routeFlights);
            LocalDate scheduleDate = date == null ? LocalDate.now(routeZone) : date;
            long upcomingFlightsOnDate = routeFlights.stream()
                    .filter(flight -> flight.departure().isAfter(Instant.now()))
                    .filter(flight -> "SCHEDULED".equals(flight.status()))
                    .filter(flight -> !Boolean.TRUE.equals(flight.templateDisabled()))
                    .filter(flight -> flight.departure().atZone(routeZone).toLocalDate().equals(scheduleDate))
                    .count();
            if (upcomingFlightsOnDate < RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.DEMO_DEPARTURES_PER_DAY) {
                List<Flight> generated = schedules.ensureDemoRouteInstances(originAirport, destinationAirport, date);
                if (!generated.isEmpty()) {
                    routeFlights = new ArrayList<>(flights.findByOriginCodeIgnoreCaseAndDestinationCodeIgnoreCase(
                            origin.trim(), destination.trim()));
                }
            }
            results = routeFlights;
        } else if ((origin == null || origin.isBlank()) && (destination == null || destination.isBlank())
                && date == null && (mode == null || mode.isBlank())
                && (countryCode == null || countryCode.isBlank())
                && (airline == null || airline.isBlank()) && maxPrice == null) {
            results = flights.findUpcomingForBrowsing(Instant.now(), SEARCHABLE_STATUSES,
                    PageRequest.of(0, SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT));
        } else {
            results = flights.findAll();
        }
        Comparator<Flight> order = switch (sort == null ? "departure" : sort.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
            case "price" -> Comparator.comparing(Flight::price);
            case "duration" -> Comparator.comparing(flight ->
                    java.time.Duration.between(flight.departure(), flight.arrival()));
            case "arrival" -> Comparator.comparing(Flight::arrival);
            default -> Comparator.comparing(Flight::departure);
        };
        ZoneId zone = timeZone(originAirport, results);
        Instant startOfDay = date == null ? null : date.atStartOfDay(zone).toInstant();
        Instant startOfNextDay = date == null ? null : date.plusDays(1).atStartOfDay(zone).toInstant();
        return results.stream()
                .filter(flight -> SEARCHABLE_STATUSES.contains(flight.status()))
                .filter(flight -> !Boolean.TRUE.equals(flight.templateDisabled()))
                .filter(flight -> flight.departure().isAfter(Instant.now()))
                .filter(flight -> origin == null || origin.isBlank()
                        || flight.originCode().equalsIgnoreCase(origin.trim()))
                .filter(flight -> destination == null || destination.isBlank()
                        || flight.destinationCode().equalsIgnoreCase(destination.trim()))
                .filter(flight -> airline == null || airline.isBlank()
                        || flight.airline().equalsIgnoreCase(airline.trim()))
                .filter(flight -> maxPrice == null || flight.price().compareTo(maxPrice) <= 0)
                .filter(flight -> startOfDay == null || (!flight.departure().isBefore(startOfDay)
                        && flight.departure().isBefore(startOfNextDay)))
                .sorted(order)
                .limit(SEARCH_RESULT_LIMIT)
                .toList();
    }

    public List<Flight> featuredDemoFlights() {
        return flights.findFeaturedDemoFlights(Instant.now(), PageRequest.of(0, 6));
    }

    private Airport airport(String code) {
        if (code == null || code.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }
        return airports.findById(code.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)).orElse(null);
    }

    private static ZoneId timeZone(Airport airport, List<Flight> flights) {
        if (airport != null && airport.timeZone() != null && !airport.timeZone().isBlank()) {
            try {
                return ZoneId.of(airport.timeZone());
            } catch (DateTimeException ignored) {
                // Fall through to the local demo schedule's default.
            }
        }
        if (!flights.isEmpty() && flights.getFirst().originTimeZone() != null
                && !flights.getFirst().originTimeZone().isBlank()) {
            try {
                return ZoneId.of(flights.getFirst().originTimeZone());
            } catch (DateTimeException ignored) {
                // Fall through to the local demo schedule's default.
            }
        }
        return ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata");
    }

    public Flight get(String id) {
        return flights.findById(id).orElseThrow(() ->
                new org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException(
                        org.springframework.http.HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Flight not found"));
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/FlightService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/FlightStatusSimulationService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.time.Instant;

import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;

@Service
public class FlightStatusSimulationService {
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;

    public FlightStatusSimulationService(MongoTemplate mongo) {
        this.mongo = mongo;
    }

    @Scheduled(fixedDelayString = "${app.flight-status-scan-ms:60000}")
    public void advanceDemoFlights() {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        mongo.updateMulti(Query.query(Criteria.where("dataSource").is("DEMO")
                        .and("status").in("SCHEDULED", "DELAYED")
                        .and("departure").lte(now)),
                Update.update("status", "DEPARTED"), Flight.class);
        mongo.updateMulti(Query.query(Criteria.where("dataSource").is("DEMO")
                        .and("status").is("DEPARTED").and("arrival").lte(now)),
                Update.update("status", "COMPLETED"), Flight.class);
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/FlightStatusSimulationService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/PaymentProcessor.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;

public interface PaymentProcessor {
    OrderResult createOrder(String userId, String bookingId);

    Booking verify(String userId, String bookingId, String orderId, String paymentId, String signature);

    DemoResult completeDemo(String userId, String bookingId, String idempotencyKey,
                            String method, boolean succeed);

    void refund(Booking booking);

    record OrderResult(String orderId, String keyId, long amount, String currency, boolean demoMode) {}
    record DemoResult(Booking booking, String reference, String status) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/PaymentProcessor.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/PaymentProcessorFacade.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;

@Service
public class PaymentProcessorFacade implements PaymentProcessor {
    private final RazorpayService razorpay;
    private final DemoPaymentService demoPayments;

    public PaymentProcessorFacade(RazorpayService razorpay, DemoPaymentService demoPayments) {
        this.razorpay = razorpay;
        this.demoPayments = demoPayments;
    }

    @Override
    public OrderResult createOrder(String userId, String bookingId) {
        RazorpayService.OrderResult order = razorpay.createOrder(userId, bookingId);
        return new OrderResult(order.orderId(), order.keyId(), order.amount(), order.currency(), order.demoMode());
    }

    @Override
    public Booking verify(String userId, String bookingId, String orderId, String paymentId, String signature) {
        return razorpay.verify(userId, bookingId, orderId, paymentId, signature);
    }

    @Override
    public DemoResult completeDemo(String userId, String bookingId, String idempotencyKey,
                                   String method, boolean succeed) {
        DemoPaymentService.PaymentResult result = demoPayments.complete(userId, bookingId,
                idempotencyKey, method, succeed);
        return new DemoResult(result.booking(), result.reference(), result.status());
    }

    @Override
    public void refund(Booking booking) {
        if (booking.razorpayPaymentId() != null) {
            razorpay.refund(booking.razorpayPaymentId(), booking.amount(), booking.currency());
        } else {
            demoPayments.refund(booking.id());
        }
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/PaymentProcessorFacade.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RazorpayService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.HexFormat;
import java.util.Map;

import javax.crypto.Mac;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

@Service
public class RazorpayService {
    private final String keyId;
    private final String keySecret;
    private final String paymentMode;
    private final RestClient restClient;
    private final BookingRepository bookings;
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;

    public RazorpayService(@Value("${razorpay.key-id}") String keyId,
                           @Value("${razorpay.key-secret}") String keySecret,
                           @Value("${payment.mode:DEMO}") String paymentMode,
                           RestClient.Builder restClientBuilder,
                           BookingRepository bookings,
                           MongoTemplate mongo) {
        this.keyId = keyId;
        this.keySecret = keySecret;
        this.paymentMode = paymentMode.toUpperCase();
        this.restClient = restClientBuilder.baseUrl("https://api.razorpay.com/v1").build();
        this.bookings = bookings;
        this.mongo = mongo;
    }

    public OrderResult createOrder(String userId, String bookingId) {
        Booking booking = ownedBooking(userId, bookingId);
        ensureNotExpired(booking);
        if (booking.status() != BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "This booking is not awaiting payment");
        }
        boolean demoMode = "DEMO".equals(paymentMode);
        if (booking.razorpayOrderId() != null) {
            if (booking.razorpayOrderId().startsWith("demo_") != demoMode) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                        "Payment mode changed after this booking was created");
            }
            return new OrderResult(booking.razorpayOrderId(), keyId,
                    booking.amount().movePointRight(2).longValueExact(), booking.currency(), demoMode);
        }
        if (demoMode) {
            Booking updated = bookings.save(booking.withPaymentOrder("demo_" + booking.id()));
            return new OrderResult(updated.razorpayOrderId(), "", updated.amount().movePointRight(2).longValueExact(),
                    updated.currency(), true);
        }
        if (!"RAZORPAY_TEST".equals(paymentMode)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, "Unknown payment mode configuration");
        }
        requireCredentials();
        try {
            JsonNode response = restClient.post().uri("/orders")
                    .headers(headers -> headers.setBasicAuth(keyId, keySecret))
                    .body(Map.of(
                            "amount", booking.amount().movePointRight(2).longValueExact(),
                            "currency", booking.currency(),
                            "receipt", booking.pnr(),
                            "notes", Map.of("bookingId", booking.id())))
                    .retrieve().body(JsonNode.class);
            if (response == null || response.path("id").asText().isBlank()) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY, "Razorpay returned an invalid order response");
            }
            Booking updated = bookings.save(booking.withPaymentOrder(response.path("id").asText()));
            return new OrderResult(updated.razorpayOrderId(), keyId,
                    updated.amount().movePointRight(2).longValueExact(), updated.currency(), false);
        } catch (ResponseStatusException exception) {
            throw exception;
        } catch (RuntimeException exception) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY, "Unable to create a Razorpay order", exception);
        }
    }

    public Booking verify(String userId, String bookingId, String orderId, String paymentId, String signature) {
        Booking booking = ownedBooking(userId, bookingId);
        ensureNotExpired(booking);
        if (!"RAZORPAY_TEST".equals(paymentMode)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Razorpay signature verification is available only in Razorpay Test Mode");
        }
        if (booking.status() == BookingStatus.CONFIRMED && paymentId.equals(booking.razorpayPaymentId())) {
            return booking;
        }
        if (booking.status() != BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT
                || booking.razorpayOrderId() == null
                || !booking.razorpayOrderId().equals(orderId)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Payment does not match this pending booking");
        }
        requireCredentials();
        Booking processing = beginVerification(booking, orderId);
        try {
            if (!isValidSignature(orderId, paymentId, signature, keySecret)) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Razorpay payment signature is invalid");
            }
            JsonNode payment = restClient.get().uri("/payments/{paymentId}", paymentId)
                    .headers(headers -> headers.setBasicAuth(keyId, keySecret))
                    .retrieve().body(JsonNode.class);
            if (payment == null
                    || !orderId.equals(payment.path("order_id").asText())
                    || !"captured".equals(payment.path("status").asText())
                    || payment.path("amount").asLong(-1) != booking.amount().movePointRight(2).longValueExact()
                    || !booking.currency().equals(payment.path("currency").asText())) {
                throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                        "Razorpay has not confirmed the full payment for this booking");
            }
            return bookings.save(processing.confirmed(paymentId));
        } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
            restorePending(processing.id(), failure);
            if (failure instanceof ResponseStatusException) {
                throw failure;
            }
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY,
                    "Unable to verify the payment status with Razorpay", failure);
        }
    }

    private Booking beginVerification(Booking booking, String orderId) {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        Criteria unexpired = new Criteria().orOperator(
                Criteria.where("expiresAt").exists(false),
                Criteria.where("expiresAt").is(null),
                Criteria.where("expiresAt").gt(now));
        Booking processing = mongo.findAndModify(
                Query.query(new Criteria().andOperator(
                        Criteria.where("_id").is(booking.id()),
                        Criteria.where("status").is(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT),
                        Criteria.where("razorpayOrderId").is(orderId),
                        unexpired)),
                Update.update("status", BookingStatus.PAYMENT_PROCESSING),
                FindAndModifyOptions.options().returnNew(true), Booking.class);
        if (processing == null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "This booking payment is expired or already being processed");
        }
        return processing;
    }

    private void restorePending(String bookingId, RuntimeException failure) {
        try {
            mongo.updateFirst(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(bookingId)
                            .and("status").is(BookingStatus.PAYMENT_PROCESSING)),
                    Update.update("status", BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT), Booking.class);
        } catch (RuntimeException restoreFailure) {
            failure.addSuppressed(restoreFailure);
        }
    }

    public void refund(String paymentId, java.math.BigDecimal amount, String currency) {
        if (paymentId == null || paymentId.isBlank()) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "The confirmed booking has no payment reference");
        }
        requireCredentials();
        try {
            restClient.post().uri("/payments/{paymentId}/refund", paymentId)
                    .headers(headers -> headers.setBasicAuth(keyId, keySecret))
                    .body(Map.of("amount", amount.movePointRight(2).longValueExact(),
                            "notes", Map.of("reason", "Customer booking cancellation")))
                    .retrieve().toBodilessEntity();
        } catch (RuntimeException exception) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY,
                    "Razorpay refund could not be completed; the booking was not cancelled", exception);
        }
    }

    public static boolean isValidSignature(String orderId, String paymentId, String signature, String secret) {
        if (orderId == null || paymentId == null || signature == null || secret == null) return false;
        try {
            Mac mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256");
            mac.init(new SecretKeySpec(secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), "HmacSHA256"));
            byte[] expected = HexFormat.of().formatHex(
                    mac.doFinal((orderId + "|" + paymentId).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)))
                    .getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);
            return MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, signature.getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
        } catch (java.security.GeneralSecurityException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("HMAC-SHA256 is unavailable", exception);
        }
    }

    private Booking ownedBooking(String userId, String bookingId) {
        return bookings.findById(bookingId)
                .filter(booking -> booking.userId().equals(userId))
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Booking not found"));
    }

    private static void ensureNotExpired(Booking booking) {
        if (booking.expiresAt() != null && !booking.expiresAt().isAfter(java.time.Instant.now())) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "This payment reservation has expired. Please create a new booking.");
        }
    }

    private void requireCredentials() {
        if (keyId.isBlank() || keySecret.isBlank()) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                    "Razorpay is not configured. Add test credentials to the server environment.");
        }
        if (!keyId.startsWith("rzp_test_")) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                    "Razorpay Test Mode requires a test key ID.");
        }
    }

    public record OrderResult(String orderId, String keyId, long amount, String currency, boolean demoMode) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RazorpayService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.ZonedDateTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;

import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.BulkOperations;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.RouteTemplate;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.SeatInventory;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.RouteTemplateRepository;

@Service
public class RollingFlightScheduleGenerator implements FlightScheduleGenerator {
    public static final int WINDOW_DAYS = 120;
    public static final int DEMO_DEPARTURES_PER_DAY = 6;
    private static final List<LocalTime> DEMO_DEPARTURE_TIMES =
            List.of(LocalTime.of(8, 0), LocalTime.of(13, 30), LocalTime.of(19, 0),
                    LocalTime.of(6, 0), LocalTime.of(15, 0), LocalTime.of(21, 0));
    private final RouteTemplateRepository templates;
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;

    public RollingFlightScheduleGenerator(RouteTemplateRepository templates, MongoTemplate mongo) {
        this.templates = templates;
        this.mongo = mongo;
    }

    @Override
    public int generateRollingWindow(LocalDate firstDate, int days) {
        if (days < 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Schedule window must contain at least one day");
        }
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        List<Flight> candidates = new ArrayList<>();
        for (RouteTemplate template : templates.findByActiveTrue()) {
            for (int offset = 0; offset < days; offset++) {
                LocalDate date = firstDate.plusDays(offset);
                if (!template.operatingDays().contains(date.getDayOfWeek().getValue())) {
                    continue;
                }
                Flight flight = createInstance(template, date);
                Instant departure = flight.departure();
                if (!departure.isAfter(now)) {
                    continue;
                }
                candidates.add(flight);
            }
        }
        if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
            return 0;
        }

        upsertFlights(candidates);
        return candidates.size();
    }

    @Override
    public List<Flight> ensureDemoRouteInstances(Airport origin, Airport destination, LocalDate requestedDate) {
        if (origin == null || destination == null || origin.code() == null || destination.code() == null
                || origin.code().equalsIgnoreCase(destination.code())
                || Boolean.FALSE.equals(origin.scheduledService())
                || Boolean.FALSE.equals(destination.scheduledService())) {
            return List.of();
        }

        ZoneId originZone = timeZone(origin);
        ZoneId destinationZone = timeZone(destination);
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now(originZone);
        LocalDate date = requestedDate == null ? today : requestedDate;
        if (requestedDate == null && DEMO_DEPARTURE_TIMES.stream()
                .noneMatch(time -> today.atTime(time).atZone(originZone).toInstant().isAfter(Instant.now()))) {
            date = today.plusDays(1);
        }
        if (date.isBefore(today) || date.isAfter(today.plusDays(WINDOW_DAYS - 1L))) {
            return List.of();
        }

        double distanceKm = distanceKm(origin, destination);
        int durationMinutes = Math.max(45, Math.min(18 * 60, (int) Math.ceil(distanceKm / 780.0 * 60) + 50));
        int flightNumberBase = 1000 + Math.floorMod(
                (origin.code() + destination.code()).hashCode(), 8000);
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        List<Flight> generated = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int index = 0; index < DEMO_DEPARTURE_TIMES.size(); index++) {
            ZonedDateTime departure = date.atTime(DEMO_DEPARTURE_TIMES.get(index)).atZone(originZone);
            if (!departure.toInstant().isAfter(now)) {
                continue;
            }
            ZonedDateTime arrival = departure.plusMinutes(durationMinutes).withZoneSameInstant(destinationZone);
            long fare = Math.max(3500L, Math.min(99999L,
                    3500L + Math.round(distanceKm * 4) + index * 700L));
            generated.add(new Flight(
                    "demo-route-" + origin.code().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT) + "-"
                            + destination.code().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT) + "-" + date + "-" + (index + 1),
                    "JetSetGo Demonstration Air",
                    "JG " + (flightNumberBase + index),
                    origin.code().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), origin.city(),
                    destination.code().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), destination.city(),
                    departure.toInstant(), arrival.toInstant(), java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(fare),
                    "INR", 48, "SCHEDULED", "DEMO", originZone.getId(), destinationZone.getId(),
                    "Illustrative demo aircraft", null, null, null, false));
        }
        upsertFlights(generated);
        return generated;
    }

    @Override
    public Flight createInstance(RouteTemplate template, LocalDate departureDate) {
        ZonedDateTime takeoff = departureDate.atTime(LocalTime.parse(template.departureLocalTime()))
                .atZone(ZoneId.of(template.originTimeZone()));
        ZonedDateTime arrival = takeoff.plusMinutes(template.durationMinutes())
                .withZoneSameInstant(ZoneId.of(template.destinationTimeZone()));
        return new Flight(template.id() + ":" + departureDate, template.airline(), template.flightNumber(),
                template.originCode(), template.originCity(), template.destinationCode(),
                template.destinationCity(), takeoff.toInstant(), arrival.toInstant(), template.baseFare(),
                template.currency(), template.capacity(), "SCHEDULED", "DEMO",
                template.originTimeZone(), template.destinationTimeZone(), template.aircraft(),
                null, null, template.id(), false);
    }

    @Override
    public int updateFutureInstances(RouteTemplate template, Set<String> protectedFlightIds) {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        List<Flight> future = mongo.find(Query.query(Criteria.where("routeTemplateId").is(template.id())
                .and("departure").gt(now)), Flight.class);
        int updated = 0;
        for (Flight existing : future) {
            if (protectedFlightIds.contains(existing.id()) || hasReservedSeats(existing.id())) {
                continue;
            }
            LocalDate departureDate = existing.departure()
                    .atZone(ZoneId.of(existing.originTimeZone())).toLocalDate();
            if (!template.operatingDays().contains(departureDate.getDayOfWeek().getValue())) {
                mongo.updateFirst(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(existing.id())),
                        Update.update("templateDisabled", true), Flight.class);
                continue;
            }
            Flight generated = createInstance(template, departureDate);
            Flight replacement = new Flight(existing.id(), generated.airline(), generated.flightNumber(),
                    generated.originCode(), generated.originCity(), generated.destinationCode(),
                    generated.destinationCity(), generated.departure(), generated.arrival(), generated.price(),
                    generated.currency(), generated.capacity(), existing.status(), generated.dataSource(),
                    generated.originTimeZone(), generated.destinationTimeZone(), generated.aircraft(),
                    generated.originTerminal(), generated.destinationTerminal(), template.id(),
                    !template.active());
            mongo.remove(Query.query(Criteria.where("flightId").is(existing.id())), SeatInventory.class);
            mongo.save(replacement);
            updated++;
        }
        return updated;
    }

    @Override
    public int setTemplateEnabled(String templateId, boolean enabled, Set<String> protectedFlightIds) {
        List<Flight> future = mongo.find(Query.query(Criteria.where("routeTemplateId").is(templateId)
                .and("departure").gt(Instant.now())), Flight.class);
        int updated = 0;
        for (Flight flight : future) {
            if (protectedFlightIds.contains(flight.id()) || hasReservedSeats(flight.id())
                    || Boolean.valueOf(!enabled).equals(flight.templateDisabled())) {
                continue;
            }
            mongo.updateFirst(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(flight.id())),
                    Update.update("templateDisabled", !enabled), Flight.class);
            updated++;
        }
        return updated;
    }

    private static ZoneId timeZone(Airport airport) {
        if (airport.timeZone() != null && !airport.timeZone().isBlank()) {
            try {
                return ZoneId.of(airport.timeZone());
            } catch (java.time.DateTimeException ignored) {
                // Use a country-based demo timezone when the catalog value is invalid.
            }
        }
        String region = airport.stateOrRegion() == null ? "" : airport.stateOrRegion().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        return switch (airport.countryCode() == null ? "" : airport.countryCode().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
            case "IN" -> ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata");
            case "AE" -> ZoneId.of("Asia/Dubai");
            case "SG" -> ZoneId.of("Asia/Singapore");
            case "GB" -> ZoneId.of("Europe/London");
            case "FR" -> ZoneId.of("Europe/Paris");
            case "JP" -> ZoneId.of("Asia/Tokyo");
            case "MY" -> ZoneId.of("Asia/Kuala_Lumpur");
            case "TH" -> ZoneId.of("Asia/Bangkok");
            case "QA" -> ZoneId.of("Asia/Qatar");
            case "US" -> region.equals("CA") || region.equals("WA") || region.equals("OR")
                    ? ZoneId.of("America/Los_Angeles")
                    : region.equals("HI") ? ZoneId.of("Pacific/Honolulu")
                    : region.equals("AK") ? ZoneId.of("America/Anchorage")
                    : region.equals("CO") || region.equals("AZ") || region.equals("UT")
                    ? ZoneId.of("America/Denver")
                    : region.equals("IL") || region.equals("TX") || region.equals("MN")
                    ? ZoneId.of("America/Chicago") : ZoneId.of("America/New_York");
            case "CA" -> region.equals("BC") ? ZoneId.of("America/Vancouver")
                    : region.equals("AB") ? ZoneId.of("America/Edmonton")
                    : ZoneId.of("America/Toronto");
            case "AU" -> region.equals("WA") ? ZoneId.of("Australia/Perth")
                    : region.equals("QLD") ? ZoneId.of("Australia/Brisbane")
                    : ZoneId.of("Australia/Sydney");
            default -> ZoneId.of("UTC");
        };
    }

    private static double distanceKm(Airport origin, Airport destination) {
        if (origin.latitude() == null || origin.longitude() == null
                || destination.latitude() == null || destination.longitude() == null) {
            return 900;
        }
        double latitudeDistance = Math.toRadians(destination.latitude() - origin.latitude());
        double longitudeDistance = Math.toRadians(destination.longitude() - origin.longitude());
        double a = Math.sin(latitudeDistance / 2) * Math.sin(latitudeDistance / 2)
                + Math.cos(Math.toRadians(origin.latitude())) * Math.cos(Math.toRadians(destination.latitude()))
                * Math.sin(longitudeDistance / 2) * Math.sin(longitudeDistance / 2);
        double clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, a));
        return 6371 * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(clamped), Math.sqrt(1 - clamped));
    }

    private void upsertFlights(List<Flight> candidates) {
        if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        BulkOperations writes = mongo.bulkOps(BulkOperations.BulkMode.UNORDERED, Flight.class);
        for (Flight flight : candidates) {
            Query byStableId = Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(flight.id()));
            writes.upsert(byStableId, new Update()
                    .setOnInsert("airline", flight.airline())
                    .setOnInsert("flightNumber", flight.flightNumber())
                    .setOnInsert("originCode", flight.originCode())
                    .setOnInsert("originCity", flight.originCity())
                    .setOnInsert("destinationCode", flight.destinationCode())
                    .setOnInsert("destinationCity", flight.destinationCity())
                    .setOnInsert("departure", flight.departure())
                    .setOnInsert("arrival", flight.arrival())
                    .setOnInsert("price", flight.price())
                    .setOnInsert("currency", flight.currency())
                    .setOnInsert("capacity", flight.capacity())
                    .setOnInsert("status", flight.status())
                    .setOnInsert("dataSource", flight.dataSource())
                    .setOnInsert("originTimeZone", flight.originTimeZone())
                    .setOnInsert("destinationTimeZone", flight.destinationTimeZone())
                    .setOnInsert("aircraft", flight.aircraft())
                    .setOnInsert("routeTemplateId", flight.routeTemplateId())
                    .setOnInsert("templateDisabled", false));
        }
        writes.execute();
    }

    private boolean hasReservedSeats(String flightId) {
        return mongo.exists(Query.query(Criteria.where("flightId").is(flightId)
                .and("available").is(false)), SeatInventory.class);
    }

    public void generateRollingWindow() {
        generateRollingWindow(LocalDate.now(), WINDOW_DAYS);
    }

    @Scheduled(cron = "0 15 0 * * *", zone = "UTC")
    public void extendScheduleDaily() {
        generateRollingWindow();
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RouteTemplateService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.DateTimeException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;

import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.RouteTemplate;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.AirportRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.RouteTemplateRepository;

@Service
public class RouteTemplateService {
    private final RouteTemplateRepository templates;
    private final AirportRepository airports;
    private final BookingRepository bookings;
    private final FlightScheduleGenerator schedules;

    public RouteTemplateService(RouteTemplateRepository templates, AirportRepository airports,
                                BookingRepository bookings, FlightScheduleGenerator schedules) {
        this.templates = templates;
        this.airports = airports;
        this.bookings = bookings;
        this.schedules = schedules;
    }

    public List<RouteTemplate> list() {
        return templates.findAll();
    }

    public RouteTemplate create(TemplateInput input) {
        RouteTemplate template = toTemplate("admin-route-" + java.util.UUID.randomUUID(), input, true);
        RouteTemplate saved = templates.save(template);
        schedules.generateRollingWindow(LocalDate.now(), RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.WINDOW_DAYS);
        return saved;
    }

    public RouteTemplate update(String id, TemplateInput input) {
        RouteTemplate current = find(id);
        RouteTemplate updated = toTemplate(id, input, current.active());
        RouteTemplate saved = templates.save(updated);
        schedules.updateFutureInstances(saved, protectedFlightIds());
        schedules.generateRollingWindow(LocalDate.now(), RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.WINDOW_DAYS);
        return saved;
    }

    public RouteTemplate setActive(String id, boolean active) {
        RouteTemplate updated = find(id).withActive(active);
        RouteTemplate saved = templates.save(updated);
        Set<String> protectedIds = protectedFlightIds();
        schedules.setTemplateEnabled(id, active, protectedIds);
        if (active) {
            schedules.generateRollingWindow(LocalDate.now(), RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.WINDOW_DAYS);
        }
        return saved;
    }

    private RouteTemplate find(String id) {
        return templates.findById(id).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Route template not found"));
    }

    private Set<String> protectedFlightIds() {
        Set<String> protectedIds = new HashSet<>();
        for (Booking booking : bookings.findAll()) {
            protectedIds.add(booking.flightId());
            if (booking.tripDetails() != null && booking.tripDetails().returnFlightId() != null) {
                protectedIds.add(booking.tripDetails().returnFlightId());
            }
        }
        return protectedIds;
    }

    private RouteTemplate toTemplate(String id, TemplateInput input, boolean active) {
        if (input == null || input.originCode() == null || input.destinationCode() == null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Route details are required");
        }
        String originCode = input.originCode().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String destinationCode = input.destinationCode().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (originCode.equals(destinationCode)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Origin and destination airports must be different");
        }
        Airport origin = airports.findById(originCode).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Origin airport is not in the airport catalog"));
        Airport destination = airports.findById(destinationCode).orElseThrow(() ->
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Destination airport is not in the airport catalog"));
        List<Integer> days = input.operatingDays() == null ? List.of() : input.operatingDays().stream()
                .distinct().sorted().toList();
        if (days.isEmpty() || days.stream().anyMatch(day -> day < 1 || day > 7)) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Choose one or more valid operating days (1=Monday through 7=Sunday)");
        }
        if (input.airline() == null || input.airline().isBlank()
                || input.flightNumber() == null || input.flightNumber().isBlank()
                || input.originCity() == null || input.originCity().isBlank()
                || input.destinationCity() == null || input.destinationCity().isBlank()
                || input.capacity() < 6 || input.capacity() > 360
                || input.durationMinutes() < 20 || input.durationMinutes() > 1800
                || input.baseFare() == null || input.baseFare().signum() <= 0) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Route template values are invalid");
        }
        try {
            LocalTime.parse(input.departureLocalTime());
            ZoneId.of(input.originTimeZone());
            ZoneId.of(input.destinationTimeZone());
        } catch (DateTimeException | NullPointerException invalidTime) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Use a valid local departure time and IANA timezones");
        }
        String currency = input.currency() == null || input.currency().isBlank()
                ? "INR" : input.currency().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!currency.matches("[A-Z]{3}")) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Currency must be a three-letter code");
        }
        return new RouteTemplate(id, input.airline().trim(), input.flightNumber().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT),
                originCode, input.originCity().trim(), destinationCode, input.destinationCity().trim(),
                input.departureLocalTime(), input.durationMinutes(), days, input.originTimeZone(),
                input.destinationTimeZone(), input.baseFare(), currency, input.capacity(),
                input.aircraft() == null || input.aircraft().isBlank() ? null : input.aircraft().trim(), active);
    }

    public record TemplateInput(String airline, String flightNumber,
                                String originCode, String originCity,
                                String destinationCode, String destinationCity,
                                String departureLocalTime, int durationMinutes,
                                List<Integer> operatingDays, String originTimeZone,
                                String destinationTimeZone, java.math.BigDecimal baseFare,
                                String currency, int capacity, String aircraft) {
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RouteTemplateService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/SeatInventoryService.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.BulkOperations;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Criteria;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.SeatInventory;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.SeatInventoryRepository;

@Service
public class SeatInventoryService {
    private final MongoTemplate mongo;
    private final SeatInventoryRepository seats;

    public SeatInventoryService(MongoTemplate mongo, SeatInventoryRepository seats) {
        this.mongo = mongo;
        this.seats = seats;
    }

    public void ensureInitialized(Flight flight) {
        List<SeatInventory> inventory = new ArrayList<>(flight.capacity());
        for (int index = 0; index < flight.capacity(); index++) {
            String number = (index / 6 + 1) + String.valueOf((char) ('A' + index % 6));
            inventory.add(new SeatInventory(SeatInventory.idFor(flight.id(), number),
                    flight.id(), number, true, cabinClass(index, flight.capacity()), null));
        }
        if (inventory.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        BulkOperations writes = mongo.bulkOps(BulkOperations.BulkMode.UNORDERED, SeatInventory.class);
        for (SeatInventory seat : inventory) {
            writes.upsert(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(seat.id())),
                    new Update().setOnInsert("flightId", seat.flightId())
                            .setOnInsert("seatNumber", seat.seatNumber())
                            .setOnInsert("available", true)
                            .setOnInsert("cabinClass", seat.cabinClass()));
            writes.updateOne(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(seat.id())
                            .and("cabinClass").exists(false)),
                    new Update().set("cabinClass", seat.cabinClass()));
        }
        writes.execute();
    }

    public List<String> availableSeats(Flight flight, String travelClass) {
        ensureInitialized(flight);
        String normalizedClass = normalizeClass(travelClass);
        return seats.findByFlightIdAndAvailableTrueOrderBySeatNumber(flight.id()).stream()
                .filter(seat -> normalizedClass.equalsIgnoreCase(
                        seat.cabinClass() == null ? "ECONOMY" : seat.cabinClass()))
                .map(SeatInventory::seatNumber)
                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(SeatInventoryService::rowNumber)
                        .thenComparing(seat -> seat.charAt(seat.length() - 1)))
                .toList();
    }

    public void release(String flightId, String seatNumber, String reservationId) {
        Criteria owner = new Criteria().orOperator(
                Criteria.where("reservationId").is(reservationId),
                Criteria.where("reservationId").exists(false),
                Criteria.where("reservationId").is(null));
        mongo.updateFirst(Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(SeatInventory.idFor(flightId, seatNumber))
                        .andOperator(owner)),
                new Update().set("available", true).unset("reservationId"), SeatInventory.class);
    }

    public void reserve(String flightId, String seatNumber, String travelClass, String reservationId) {
        String normalizedClass = normalizeClass(travelClass);
        SeatInventory reserved = mongo.findAndModify(
                Query.query(Criteria.where("_id").is(SeatInventory.idFor(flightId, seatNumber))
                        .and("available").is(true).and("cabinClass").is(normalizedClass)),
                new Update().set("available", false).set("reservationId", reservationId),
                org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions.options().returnNew(true),
                SeatInventory.class);
        if (reserved == null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Seat " + seatNumber + " is unavailable in " + normalizedClass.replace('_', ' ') + " class");
        }
    }

    static String cabinClass(int index, int capacity) {
        if (capacity < 30) {
            return "ECONOMY";
        }
        int row = index / 6 + 1;
        if (row == 1) return "FIRST";
        if (row <= 3) return "BUSINESS";
        if (row == 4) return "PREMIUM_ECONOMY";
        return "ECONOMY";
    }

    private static String normalizeClass(String travelClass) {
        if (travelClass == null || travelClass.isBlank()) {
            return "ECONOMY";
        }
        return travelClass.trim().toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
    }

    private static int rowNumber(String seat) {
        return Integer.parseInt(seat.substring(0, seat.length() - 1));
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/SeatInventoryService.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/AbstractFarePolicy.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service.fare;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

public abstract class AbstractFarePolicy implements FarePolicy {
    private final String travelClass;

    protected AbstractFarePolicy(String travelClass) {
        this.travelClass = travelClass;
    }

    @Override
    public final String travelClass() {
        return travelClass;
    }

    @Override
    public final BigDecimal multiplier() {
        return classMultiplier();
    }

    @Override
    public final BigDecimal calculate(BigDecimal baseFare, int passengerCount) {
        return baseFare.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(passengerCount)).multiply(multiplier());
    }

    protected abstract BigDecimal classMultiplier();
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/AbstractFarePolicy.java =====

public final class BusinessFarePolicy extends AbstractFarePolicy {
    public BusinessFarePolicy() {
        super("BUSINESS");
    }

    @Override
    protected BigDecimal classMultiplier() {
        return new BigDecimal("2.5");
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/FarePolicy.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service.fare;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

public interface FarePolicy {
    String travelClass();

    BigDecimal multiplier();

    BigDecimal calculate(BigDecimal baseFare, int passengerCount);
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/FarePolicy.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/FarePolicyResolver.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service.fare;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

@Component
public class FarePolicyResolver {
    private final Map<String, FarePolicy> policies;

    public FarePolicyResolver(List<FarePolicy> policies) {
        this.policies = policies.stream().collect(Collectors.toUnmodifiableMap(
                policy -> policy.travelClass().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), Function.identity()));
    }

    public FarePolicy resolve(String travelClass) {
        String normalized = travelClass == null || travelClass.isBlank()
                ? "ECONOMY" : travelClass.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        FarePolicy policy = policies.get(normalized);
        if (policy == null) {
            throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Unsupported travel class");
        }
        return policy;
    }

    public BigDecimal calculate(String travelClass, BigDecimal baseFare, int passengerCount) {
        return resolve(travelClass).calculate(baseFare, passengerCount).setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/FarePolicyResolver.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/AdminController.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.DecimalMin;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Max;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Min;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.DeleteMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PatchMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.format.annotation.DateTimeFormat;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.RouteTemplate;
import com.jetsetgo.service.AdminService;
import com.jetsetgo.service.RouteTemplateService;
import com.jetsetgo.web.dto.BookingResponse;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/admin")
public class AdminController {
    private final AdminService admin;
    private final RouteTemplateService routeTemplates;

    public AdminController(AdminService admin, RouteTemplateService routeTemplates) {
        this.admin = admin;
        this.routeTemplates = routeTemplates;
    }

    @GetMapping("/summary")
    public AdminService.Summary summary() {
        return admin.summary();
    }

    @GetMapping("/flights")
    public List<Flight> flights(@RequestParam(required = false) String from,
                                @RequestParam(required = false) String to,
                                @RequestParam(required = false)
                                @DateTimeFormat(iso = DateTimeFormat.ISO.DATE) LocalDate date,
                                @RequestParam(required = false) String flightNumber) {
        return admin.flights(from, to, date, flightNumber);
    }

    @GetMapping("/routes")
    public List<RouteTemplate> routeTemplates() {
        return routeTemplates.list();
    }

    @PostMapping("/routes")
    public RouteTemplate createRoute(@Valid @RequestBody RouteTemplateRequest request) {
        return routeTemplates.create(request.toInput());
    }

    @PutMapping("/routes/{id}")
    public RouteTemplate updateRoute(@PathVariable String id,
                                     @Valid @RequestBody RouteTemplateRequest request) {
        return routeTemplates.update(id, request.toInput());
    }

    @PatchMapping("/routes/{id}/active")
    public RouteTemplate setRouteActive(@PathVariable String id,
                                        @Valid @RequestBody ActiveRequest request) {
        return routeTemplates.setActive(id, request.active());
    }

    @PostMapping("/flights")
    public Flight addFlight(@Valid @RequestBody FlightRequest request) {
        return admin.addFlight(new AdminService.NewFlight(request.airline(), request.flightNumber(),
                request.originCode(), request.originCity(), request.destinationCode(),
                request.destinationCity(), request.departure(), request.arrival(),
                request.price(), request.capacity(), request.originTimeZone(), request.destinationTimeZone(),
                request.aircraft(), request.originTerminal(), request.destinationTerminal()));
    }

    @PutMapping("/flights/{id}")
    public Flight updateFlight(@PathVariable String id, @Valid @RequestBody FlightRequest request) {
        return admin.updateFlight(id, new AdminService.NewFlight(request.airline(), request.flightNumber(),
                request.originCode(), request.originCity(), request.destinationCode(),
                request.destinationCity(), request.departure(), request.arrival(),
                request.price(), request.capacity(), request.originTimeZone(), request.destinationTimeZone(),
                request.aircraft(), request.originTerminal(), request.destinationTerminal()));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/flights/{id}/status")
    public Flight changeStatus(@PathVariable String id, @Valid @RequestBody StatusRequest request) {
        return admin.changeStatus(id, request.status());
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/flights/{id}")
    public void deleteFlight(@PathVariable String id) {
        admin.deleteFlight(id);
    }

    @GetMapping("/bookings")
    public List<BookingResponse> bookings(@RequestParam(required = false) String status) {
        return admin.bookings(status).stream().map(BookingResponse::from).toList();
    }

    @PostMapping("/bookings/{id}/cancel")
    public BookingResponse cancelBooking(@PathVariable String id) {
        return BookingResponse.from(admin.cancelBooking(id));
    }

    @GetMapping("/users")
    public List<AdminService.UserSummary> users() {
        return admin.users();
    }

    public record FlightRequest(@NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String airline,
                                @NotBlank @Size(max = 12) String flightNumber,
                                @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "[A-Za-z]{3}") String originCode,
                                @NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String originCity,
                                @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "[A-Za-z]{3}") String destinationCode,
                                @NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String destinationCity,
                                @NotNull Instant departure, @NotNull Instant arrival,
                                @NotNull @DecimalMin("1.00") BigDecimal price,
                                @Min(6) @Max(360) int capacity,
                                String originTimeZone, String destinationTimeZone,
                                @Size(max = 100) String aircraft,
                                @Size(max = 40) String originTerminal,
                                @Size(max = 40) String destinationTerminal) {}
    public record StatusRequest(@NotBlank String status) {}
    public record ActiveRequest(boolean active) {}
    public record RouteTemplateRequest(@NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String airline,
                                       @NotBlank @Size(max = 12) String flightNumber,
                                       @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "[A-Za-z]{3}") String originCode,
                                       @NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String originCity,
                                       @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "[A-Za-z]{3}") String destinationCode,
                                       @NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String destinationCity,
                                       @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "(?:[01][0-9]|2[0-3]):[0-5][0-9]") String departureLocalTime,
                                       @Min(20) @Max(1800) int durationMinutes,
                                       @Size(min = 1, max = 7) List<@Min(1) @Max(7) Integer> operatingDays,
                                       @NotBlank String originTimeZone,
                                       @NotBlank String destinationTimeZone,
                                       @NotNull @DecimalMin("1.00") BigDecimal baseFare,
                                       @Pattern(regexp = "[A-Za-z]{3}") String currency,
                                       @Min(6) @Max(360) int capacity,
                                       @Size(max = 100) String aircraft) {
        private com.jetsetgo.service.RouteTemplateService.TemplateInput toInput() {
            return new com.jetsetgo.service.RouteTemplateService.TemplateInput(airline, flightNumber,
                    originCode, originCity, destinationCode, destinationCity, departureLocalTime,
                    durationMinutes, operatingDays, originTimeZone, destinationTimeZone,
                    baseFare, currency, capacity, aircraft);
        }
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/AdminController.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/AirportController.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.service.AirportService;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/airports")
public class AirportController {
    private final AirportService airports;

    public AirportController(AirportService airports) {
        this.airports = airports;
    }

    @GetMapping
    public List<Airport> search(@RequestParam(required = false) String q,
                                @RequestParam(required = false) String countryCode,
                                @RequestParam(defaultValue = "false") boolean scheduledOnly) {
        return airports.search(q, countryCode, scheduledOnly);
    }

    @GetMapping("/countries")
    public List<String> countries() {
        return airports.countryCodes();
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/AirportController.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/ApiExceptionHandler.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import java.util.Objects;

import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ProblemDetail;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MethodArgumentNotValidException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

@RestControllerAdvice
public class ApiExceptionHandler {
    @ExceptionHandler(ResponseStatusException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ProblemDetail> handleResponseStatus(ResponseStatusException exception) {
        String detail = exception.getReason();
        if (detail == null || detail.isBlank()) {
            detail = "The request could not be completed.";
        }
        ProblemDetail problem = ProblemDetail.forStatusAndDetail(exception.getStatusCode(), detail);
        return ResponseEntity.status(exception.getStatusCode()).body(problem);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    public ResponseEntity<ProblemDetail> handleValidation(MethodArgumentNotValidException exception) {
        String detail = exception.getBindingResult().getFieldErrors().stream()
                .map(error -> error.getDefaultMessage())
                .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                .findFirst()
                .orElse("Check the submitted fields.");
        ProblemDetail problem = ProblemDetail.forStatusAndDetail(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, detail);
        return ResponseEntity.badRequest().body(problem);
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/ApiExceptionHandler.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/AuthController.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import com.jetsetgo.service.AuthService;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/auth")
public class AuthController {
    private final AuthService auth;

    public AuthController(AuthService auth) {
        this.auth = auth;
    }

    @PostMapping("/register")
    public AuthService.AuthResult register(@Valid @RequestBody RegisterRequest request) {
        return auth.register(request.name(), request.email(), request.password());
    }

    @PostMapping("/login")
    public AuthService.AuthResult login(@Valid @RequestBody LoginRequest request) {
        return auth.login(request.email(), request.password());
    }

    public record RegisterRequest(@NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String name,
                                  @NotBlank @Email @Size(max = 254) String email,
                                  @NotBlank @Size(min = 10, max = 72) String password) {}
    public record LoginRequest(@NotBlank @Email String email, @NotBlank String password) {}
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/BookingController.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import java.util.List;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Email;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import com.jetsetgo.service.BookingService;
import com.jetsetgo.web.dto.BookingResponse;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/bookings")
public class BookingController {
    private final BookingService bookings;

    public BookingController(BookingService bookings) {
        this.bookings = bookings;
    }

    @PostMapping
    public BookingResponse create(Authentication authentication,
                                   @RequestHeader("Idempotency-Key") @Size(min = 8, max = 100) String key,
                                   @Valid @RequestBody BookingRequest request) {
        List<BookingService.PassengerInput> passengers = request.passengers() == null ? null
                : request.passengers().stream().map(passenger -> new BookingService.PassengerInput(
                        passenger.name(), passenger.nationality(), passenger.passportExpiryDate())).toList();
        return BookingResponse.from(bookings.create(authentication.getName(), key,
                new BookingService.CreateBooking(request.flightId(), request.seatNumber(),
                        request.passengerName(), request.passengerEmail(), request.returnFlightId(),
                        request.seatNumbers(), request.returnSeatNumbers(), passengers,
                        request.travelClass())));
    }

    @GetMapping
    public List<BookingResponse> list(Authentication authentication) {
        return bookings.forUser(authentication.getName()).stream().map(BookingResponse::from).toList();
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public BookingResponse get(Authentication authentication, @PathVariable String id) {
        return BookingResponse.from(bookings.getForUser(authentication.getName(), id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    public BookingResponse cancel(Authentication authentication, @PathVariable String id) {
        return BookingResponse.from(bookings.cancel(authentication.getName(), id));
    }

    public record BookingRequest(@NotBlank String flightId,
                                 @NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "[1-9][A-F]") String seatNumber,
                                 @NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String passengerName,
                                 @NotBlank @Email @Size(max = 254) String passengerEmail,
                                 String returnFlightId,
                                 @Size(max = 6) List<@NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "[1-9][A-F]") String> seatNumbers,
                                 @Size(max = 6) List<@NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "[1-9][A-F]") String> returnSeatNumbers,
                                 @Size(max = 6) List<@Valid PassengerRequest> passengers,
                                 String travelClass) {}

    public record PassengerRequest(@NotBlank @Size(max = 100) String name,
                                   @Size(max = 2) String nationality,
                                   java.time.LocalDate passportExpiryDate) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/BookingController.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/FlightController.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

import org.springframework.format.annotation.DateTimeFormat;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.service.FlightService;
import com.jetsetgo.service.SeatInventoryService;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api")
public class FlightController {
    private final FlightService flights;
    private final SeatInventoryService seats;

    public FlightController(FlightService flights, SeatInventoryService seats) {
        this.flights = flights;
        this.seats = seats;
    }

    @GetMapping("/health")
    public Health health() {
        return new Health("ok");
    }

    @GetMapping("/flights")
    public List<Flight> search(@RequestParam(required = false) String from,
                               @RequestParam(required = false) String to,
                               @RequestParam(required = false)
                               @DateTimeFormat(iso = DateTimeFormat.ISO.DATE) LocalDate date,
                               @RequestParam(defaultValue = "departure") String sort,
                               @RequestParam(required = false) String mode,
                               @RequestParam(required = false) String countryCode,
                               @RequestParam(required = false) String airline,
                               @RequestParam(required = false) BigDecimal maxPrice) {
        return flights.search(from, to, date, sort, mode, countryCode, airline, maxPrice);
    }

    @GetMapping("/flights/featured")
    public List<Flight> featured() {
        return flights.featuredDemoFlights();
    }

    @GetMapping("/flights/{id}")
    public Flight get(@PathVariable String id) {
        return flights.get(id);
    }

    @GetMapping("/flights/{id}/seats")
    public List<String> availableSeats(@PathVariable String id,
                                       @RequestParam(defaultValue = "ECONOMY") String travelClass) {
        return seats.availableSeats(flights.get(id), travelClass);
    }

    public record Health(String status) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/FlightController.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/PaymentController.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;
import org.springframework.security.core.Authentication;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import com.jetsetgo.service.PaymentProcessor;
import com.jetsetgo.web.dto.BookingResponse;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/bookings/{bookingId}/payments")
public class PaymentController {
    private final PaymentProcessor payments;

    public PaymentController(PaymentProcessor payments) {
        this.payments = payments;
    }

    @PostMapping("/order")
    public PaymentProcessor.OrderResult createOrder(Authentication authentication, @PathVariable String bookingId) {
        return payments.createOrder(authentication.getName(), bookingId);
    }

    @PostMapping("/verify")
    public BookingResponse verify(Authentication authentication, @PathVariable String bookingId,
                                  @Valid @RequestBody VerifyPaymentRequest request) {
        return BookingResponse.from(payments.verify(authentication.getName(), bookingId,
                request.razorpayOrderId(), request.razorpayPaymentId(), request.razorpaySignature()));
    }

    @PostMapping("/demo/complete")
    public DemoPaymentResponse completeDemo(Authentication authentication, @PathVariable String bookingId,
                                             @RequestHeader("Idempotency-Key") @Size(min = 8, max = 100) String key,
                                             @Valid @RequestBody DemoPaymentRequest request) {
        PaymentProcessor.DemoResult result = payments.completeDemo(authentication.getName(), bookingId,
                key, request.method(), request.succeed());
        return new DemoPaymentResponse(BookingResponse.from(result.booking()), result.reference(), result.status());
    }

    public record VerifyPaymentRequest(@NotBlank String razorpayOrderId,
                                      @NotBlank String razorpayPaymentId,
                                      @NotBlank String razorpaySignature) {}
    public record DemoPaymentRequest(@NotBlank @Pattern(regexp = "UPI|CARD|NET_BANKING|WALLET") String method,
                                     boolean succeed) {}
    public record DemoPaymentResponse(BookingResponse booking, String reference, String status) {}
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/main/java/com/jetsetgo/web/PaymentController.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web.dto;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking.TripDetails;

public record BookingResponse(String id, String pnr, String flightId, String seatNumber,
                              String passengerName, String passengerEmail, BigDecimal amount,
                              String currency, BookingStatus status, Instant createdAt,
                              TripDetails tripDetails, Instant expiresAt) {
    public static BookingResponse from(Booking booking) {
        return new BookingResponse(booking.id(), booking.pnr(), booking.flightId(),
                booking.seatNumber(), booking.passengerName(), booking.passengerEmail(),
                booking.amount(), booking.currency(), booking.status(), booking.createdAt(),
                booking.tripDetails(), booking.expiresAt());
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/config/DemoDataTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.config;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

import java.util.List;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.AirportRepository;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class DemoDataTest {
    @Mock AirportRepository airports;

    @Test
    void seedsMissingAirportsAndRepairsIncompleteRecordsWithoutReplacingImportedOnes() {
        Airport incompleteDelhi = new Airport("DEL", "Delhi", "Indira Gandhi International Airport",
                "", "India", "", "Asia/Kolkata", true, "large_airport", null, null, null);
        Airport importedMumbai = new Airport("BOM", "Mumbai", "Chhatrapati Shivaji Maharaj International Airport",
                "Maharashtra", "India", "IN", null, true, "large_airport", null, null, "OURAIRPORTS");
        when(airports.findAllById(any())).thenReturn(List.of(incompleteDelhi, importedMumbai));

        List<Airport> seeded = DemoData.seedCuratedAirports(airports);

        Airport repairedDelhi = seeded.stream()
                .filter(airport -> airport.code().equals("DEL"))
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow();
        assertEquals("IN", repairedDelhi.countryCode());
        assertEquals("CURATED", repairedDelhi.dataSource());
        assertTrue(seeded.stream().anyMatch(airport -> airport.code().equals("LKO")));
        assertFalse(seeded.stream().anyMatch(airport -> airport.code().equals("BOM")));
        verify(airports).saveAll(seeded);
    }
}
