// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/domain/BookingTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.domain;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class BookingTest {
    @Test
    void paymentAndCancellationTransitionsPreserveBookingDetails() {
        Booking pending = new Booking("id", "PNR12345", "user", "flight", "2A",
                "A. Passenger", "passenger@example.com", new BigDecimal("6499"), "INR",
                BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, "order_1", null, Instant.EPOCH, "key");

        Booking confirmed = pending.confirmed("pay_1");
        Booking cancelled = confirmed.cancelled();

        assertEquals(BookingStatus.CONFIRMED, confirmed.status());
        assertEquals("pay_1", confirmed.razorpayPaymentId());
        assertEquals(BookingStatus.CANCELLED, cancelled.status());
        assertEquals(pending.pnr(), cancelled.pnr());
        assertEquals(pending.amount(), cancelled.amount());
        assertEquals(pending.seatNumber(), cancelled.seatNumber());
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AirportDatasetImporterTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.StringReader;
import java.util.Map;

import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVFormat;
import org.apache.commons.csv.CSVParser;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;

class AirportDatasetImporterTest {
    @Test
    void mapsVerifiedIataAirportsAndKeepsScheduledServiceStatus() throws Exception {
        String csv = """
                type,iata_code,municipality,name,iso_country,iso_region,scheduled_service,latitude_deg,longitude_deg
                large_airport,ABC,Sample City,"Sample, International Airport",US,US-CA,yes,34.1,-118.4
                small_airport,ABC,Sample City,Alternate Field,US,US-CA,no,34.2,-118.5
                medium_airport,DEF,Other City,Other Airport,CA,CA-ON,no,43.6,-79.4
                heliport,HEL,Sample City,Heliport,US,US-CA,yes,34.0,-118.3
                small_airport,,Sample City,No IATA Code,US,US-CA,no,34.0,-118.3
                """;
        try (CSVParser parser = CSVFormat.DEFAULT.builder().setHeader().setSkipHeaderRecord(true)
                .build().parse(new StringReader(csv))) {
            Map<String, Airport> airports = AirportDatasetImporter.mapAirportRecords(
                    parser, Map.of("US", "United States", "CA", "Canada"),
                    Map.of("US-CA", "California", "CA-ON", "Ontario"));

            assertEquals(2, airports.size());
            assertEquals("Sample, International Airport", airports.get("ABC").name());
            assertEquals("California", airports.get("ABC").stateOrRegion());
            assertEquals("United States", airports.get("ABC").country());
            assertEquals("US", airports.get("ABC").countryCode());
            assertTrue(airports.get("ABC").scheduledService());
            assertFalse(airports.get("DEF").scheduledService());
            assertEquals("OURAIRPORTS", airports.get("DEF").dataSource());
        }
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AirportDatasetImporterTest.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AirportServiceTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

import java.util.List;

import org.bson.Document;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class AirportServiceTest {
    @Mock MongoTemplate mongo;
    @InjectMocks AirportService service;

    @Test
    void multiWordSearchMatchesEachTermAcrossAirportFields() {
        when(mongo.find(any(Query.class), eq(Airport.class))).thenReturn(List.of());

        service.search("Allahabad Airport", "IN", false);

        ArgumentCaptor<Query> query = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(Query.class);
        verify(mongo).find(query.capture(), eq(Airport.class));
        Document queryObject = query.getValue().getQueryObject();
        List<?> filters = queryObject.getList("$and", Object.class);

        assertEquals(3, filters.size());
        assertTrue(((Document) filters.get(0)).containsKey("$or"));
        assertTrue(((Document) filters.get(1)).containsKey("$or"));
        assertTrue(queryObject.toJson().contains("Allahabad"));
        assertTrue(queryObject.toJson().contains("Airport"));
        assertEquals("IN", ((Document) filters.get(2)).getString("countryCode"));
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AuthServiceTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

import java.util.Optional;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.config.JwtService;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.AppUser;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.UserRepository;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class AuthServiceTest {
    @Mock UserRepository users;
    @Mock PasswordEncoder passwords;
    @Mock JwtService tokens;
    @InjectMocks AuthService service;

    @Test
    void registrationPersistsNormalizedEmailAndEncodedPassword() {
        when(users.existsByEmailIgnoreCase("traveler@example.com")).thenReturn(false);
        when(passwords.encode("correct-password")).thenReturn("bcrypt-hash");
        when(users.save(any(AppUser.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> invocation.getArgument(0));
        when(tokens.issue(any(AppUser.class))).thenReturn("session-token");

        service.register("  Traveler  ", " Traveler@Example.com ", "correct-password");

        ArgumentCaptor<AppUser> savedUser = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(AppUser.class);
        verify(users).save(savedUser.capture());
        assertEquals("traveler@example.com", savedUser.getValue().email());
        assertEquals("bcrypt-hash", savedUser.getValue().passwordHash());
        assertEquals("Traveler", savedUser.getValue().name());
        assertEquals(AppUser.Role.CUSTOMER, savedUser.getValue().role());
    }

    @Test
    void wrongPasswordReturnsClearGenericAuthenticationError() {
        AppUser user = new AppUser("user-1", "traveler@example.com", "bcrypt-hash",
                "Traveler", AppUser.Role.CUSTOMER);
        when(users.findByEmailIgnoreCase("traveler@example.com")).thenReturn(Optional.of(user));
        when(passwords.matches("wrong-password", "bcrypt-hash")).thenReturn(false);

        ResponseStatusException failure = assertThrows(ResponseStatusException.class,
                () -> service.login("traveler@example.com", "wrong-password"));

        assertEquals(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, failure.getStatusCode());
        assertEquals("Email or password is incorrect. Check your details and try again.", failure.getReason());
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/AuthServiceTest.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/BookingExpiryServiceTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class BookingExpiryServiceTest {
    @Mock BookingRepository bookings;
    @Mock MongoTemplate mongo;
    @Mock SeatInventoryService seats;
    @InjectMocks BookingExpiryService service;

    @Test
    void releasesEveryLegOnlyAfterPendingBookingIsAtomicallyExpired() {
        Booking.TripDetails trip = new Booking.TripDetails("return-flight", List.of("10A"),
                List.of("24F"), "ECONOMY", List.of());
        Booking booking = new Booking("booking-id", "JSG12345", "user", "outbound-flight",
                "10A", "Passenger", "p@example.com", new BigDecimal("1000"), "INR",
                BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, null, null, Instant.now().minusSeconds(1200),
                "request-key", trip, Instant.now().minusSeconds(300));
        when(bookings.findByStatusAndExpiresAtBefore(eq(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT), any()))
                .thenReturn(List.of(booking));
        when(mongo.findAndModify(any(Query.class), any(Update.class), any(FindAndModifyOptions.class),
                eq(Booking.class))).thenReturn(booking.expired());

        service.releaseExpiredReservations();

        verify(seats).release("outbound-flight", "10A", "booking-id");
        verify(seats).release("return-flight", "24F", "booking-id");
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotReleaseSeatsWhenPaymentWonTheStateTransitionRace() {
        Booking booking = new Booking("booking-id", "JSG12345", "user", "outbound-flight",
                "1A", "Passenger", "p@example.com", new BigDecimal("1000"), "INR",
                BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, null, null, Instant.now().minusSeconds(1200),
                "request-key", null, Instant.now().minusSeconds(300));
        when(bookings.findByStatusAndExpiresAtBefore(eq(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT), any()))
                .thenReturn(List.of(booking));
        when(mongo.findAndModify(any(Query.class), any(Update.class), any(FindAndModifyOptions.class),
                eq(Booking.class))).thenReturn(null);

        service.releaseExpiredReservations();

        org.mockito.Mockito.verifyNoInteractions(seats);
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/BookingServiceTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyString;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.doThrow;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.doNothing;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.AirportRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.FlightRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.service.fare.BusinessFarePolicy;
import com.jetsetgo.service.fare.FarePolicyResolver;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class BookingServiceTest {
    @Mock BookingRepository bookings;
    @Mock FlightRepository flights;
    @Mock AirportRepository airports;
    @Mock MongoTemplate mongo;
    @Mock PaymentProcessor payments;
    @Mock SeatInventoryService seatInventory;
    @Mock FarePolicyResolver farePolicies;
    @InjectMocks BookingService service;

    @Test
    void createsRoundTripForMultiplePassengersAndPricesSelectedClass() {
        Flight outbound = flight("out", "DEL", "DXB", 3, "1000");
        Flight returning = flight("return", "DXB", "DEL", 7, "1500");
        when(flights.findById("out")).thenReturn(Optional.of(outbound));
        when(flights.findById("return")).thenReturn(Optional.of(returning));
        when(airports.findById("DEL")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DEL", "IN")));
        when(airports.findById("DXB")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DXB", "AE")));
        when(bookings.findByUserIdAndIdempotencyKey("user", "request-key")).thenReturn(Optional.empty());
        when(farePolicies.resolve("BUSINESS")).thenReturn(new BusinessFarePolicy());
        when(farePolicies.calculate("BUSINESS", new BigDecimal("2500"), 2))
                .thenReturn(new BigDecimal("12500.00"));
        when(bookings.save(any(Booking.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> invocation.getArgument(0));

        Booking booking = service.create("user", "request-key", new BookingService.CreateBooking(
                "out", "1A", "A. Passenger", "passenger@example.com", "return",
                List.of("1A", "1B"), List.of("2A", "2B"),
                List.of(
                        new BookingService.PassengerInput("A. Passenger", "IN", LocalDate.now().plusYears(2)),
                        new BookingService.PassengerInput("B. Passenger", "CA", LocalDate.now().plusYears(2))),
                "BUSINESS"));

        assertEquals(new BigDecimal("12500.00"), booking.amount());
        assertEquals("return", booking.tripDetails().returnFlightId());
        assertEquals(List.of("1A", "1B"), booking.tripDetails().seatNumbers());
        assertEquals(List.of("2A", "2B"), booking.tripDetails().returnSeatNumbers());
        assertEquals("BUSINESS", booking.tripDetails().travelClass());
        assertEquals(2, booking.tripDetails().passengers().size());
    }

    @Test
    void releasesOutboundSeatIfReturnSeatReservationFails() {
        Flight outbound = flight("out", "DEL", "DXB", 3, "1000");
        Flight returning = flight("return", "DXB", "DEL", 7, "1500");
        when(flights.findById("out")).thenReturn(Optional.of(outbound));
        when(flights.findById("return")).thenReturn(Optional.of(returning));
        when(airports.findById("DEL")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DEL", "IN")));
        when(airports.findById("DXB")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DXB", "AE")));
        when(bookings.findByUserIdAndIdempotencyKey("user", "request-key")).thenReturn(Optional.empty());
        when(farePolicies.resolve("ECONOMY")).thenReturn(new com.jetsetgo.service.fare.EconomyFarePolicy());
        doNothing().doThrow(new ResponseStatusException(org.springframework.http.HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "taken"))
                .when(seatInventory).reserve(anyString(), anyString(), eq("ECONOMY"), anyString());

        assertThrows(ResponseStatusException.class, () -> service.create("user", "request-key",
                new BookingService.CreateBooking("out", "1A", "A. Passenger", "passenger@example.com",
                        "return", List.of("1A"), List.of("2A"),
                        List.of(new BookingService.PassengerInput(
                                "A. Passenger", "IN", LocalDate.now().plusYears(2))), "ECONOMY")));

        verify(seatInventory).release(eq("out"), eq("1A"), any());
    }

    private static Flight flight(String id, String origin, String destination, int days, String price) {
        Instant departure = Instant.now().plusSeconds(days * 24L * 60 * 60);
        return new Flight(id, "Demo Air", id, origin, origin, destination, destination,
                departure, departure.plusSeconds(3 * 60 * 60), new BigDecimal(price),
                "INR", 48, "SCHEDULED", "DEMO");
    }

    private static Airport airport(String code, String countryCode) {
        return new Airport(code, code, code + " airport", "", countryCode, countryCode,
                "UTC", true, "large_airport", null, null, "TEST");
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/DemoPaymentServiceTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.DemoPayment;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.DemoPaymentRepository;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class DemoPaymentServiceTest {
    @Mock BookingRepository bookings;
    @Mock DemoPaymentRepository payments;
    @Mock MongoTemplate mongo;
    DemoPaymentService service;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        service = new DemoPaymentService(bookings, payments, mongo, "DEMO");
    }

    @Test
    void successfulDemoPaymentConfirmsBookingWithoutPaymentCredentials() {
        Booking pending = booking(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT);
        Booking processing = booking(BookingStatus.PAYMENT_PROCESSING);
        Booking confirmed = booking(BookingStatus.CONFIRMED);
        when(payments.findByUserIdAndIdempotencyKey("user", "attempt-123")).thenReturn(Optional.empty());
        when(bookings.findById("booking")).thenReturn(Optional.of(pending));
        when(mongo.findAndModify(any(Query.class), any(Update.class), any(FindAndModifyOptions.class),
                eq(Booking.class))).thenReturn(processing);
        when(payments.save(any(DemoPayment.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> invocation.getArgument(0));
        when(bookings.save(any(Booking.class))).thenReturn(confirmed);

        DemoPaymentService.PaymentResult result =
                service.complete("user", "booking", "attempt-123", "UPI", true);

        assertEquals("SUCCEEDED", result.status());
        assertEquals(BookingStatus.CONFIRMED, result.booking().status());
        verify(bookings).save(any(Booking.class));
    }

    @Test
    void failedDemoPaymentLeavesBookingPending() {
        Booking pending = booking(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT);
        when(payments.findByUserIdAndIdempotencyKey("user", "attempt-456")).thenReturn(Optional.empty());
        when(bookings.findById("booking")).thenReturn(Optional.of(pending));
        when(payments.save(any(DemoPayment.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> invocation.getArgument(0));

        DemoPaymentService.PaymentResult result =
                service.complete("user", "booking", "attempt-456", "CARD", false);

        assertEquals("FAILED", result.status());
        assertEquals(BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, result.booking().status());
        verify(mongo, never()).findAndModify(any(Query.class), any(Update.class),
                any(FindAndModifyOptions.class), eq(Booking.class));
        verify(bookings, never()).save(any(Booking.class));
    }

    private static Booking booking(BookingStatus status) {
        return new Booking("booking", "JSG12345", "user", "flight", "2A",
                "A Passenger", "passenger@example.com", new BigDecimal("5000"), "INR",
                status, "demo_booking", null, Instant.EPOCH, "create-key");
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/FlightServiceTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.IntStream;

import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.AirportRepository;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.FlightRepository;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class FlightServiceTest {
    @Mock FlightRepository repository;
    @Mock AirportRepository airportRepository;
    @Mock FlightScheduleGenerator schedules;
    @InjectMocks FlightService service;

    @Test
    void appliesOneSidedAirportFiltersAndIncludesDelayedCustomerStatus() {
        Flight delToBom = flight("a", "DEL", "BOM", "SCHEDULED");
        Flight bomToBlr = flight("b", "BOM", "BLR", "SCHEDULED");
        Flight delayed = flight("c", "DEL", "BLR", "DELAYED");
        when(repository.findAll()).thenReturn(List.of(delToBom, bomToBlr, delayed));

        assertEquals(List.of(delToBom, delayed), service.search("DEL", null, null, null, null, null, null, null));
        assertEquals(List.of(bomToBlr, delayed), service.search(null, "BLR", null, null, null, null, null, null));
        assertEquals(List.of(delToBom, bomToBlr, delayed), service.search(null, null,
                java.time.LocalDate.of(2030, 1, 1), null, null, null, null, null));
    }

    @Test
    void domesticSearchRequiresBothAirportsToBeInSelectedCountry() {
        Flight delToBom = flight("a", "DEL", "BOM", "SCHEDULED");
        when(airportRepository.findById("DEL")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DEL", "IN")));
        when(airportRepository.findById("BOM")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("BOM", "IN")));
        when(repository.findByOriginCodeIgnoreCaseAndDestinationCodeIgnoreCase("DEL", "BOM"))
                .thenReturn(List.of(delToBom));

        assertEquals(List.of(delToBom), service.search(
                "DEL", "BOM", null, "departure", "domestic", "IN", null, null));
        assertEquals(List.of(), service.search(
                "DEL", "BOM", null, "departure", "domestic", "US", null, null));
    }

    @Test
    void internationalSearchRejectsSameCountryRoutes() {
        when(airportRepository.findById("DEL")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DEL", "IN")));
        when(airportRepository.findById("BOM")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("BOM", "IN")));

        assertEquals(List.of(), service.search(
                "DEL", "BOM", null, "departure", "international", null, null, null));
    }

    @Test
    void internationalSearchAllowsDifferentCountriesAndAppliesAirlineAndFareFilters() {
        Flight airIndia = new Flight("a", "Air India", "AI 1", "DEL", "Delhi", "DXB", "Dubai",
                Instant.parse("2030-01-01T10:00:00Z"), Instant.parse("2030-01-01T12:00:00Z"),
                new BigDecimal("1400"), "INR", 10, "SCHEDULED", "DEMO");
        Flight otherAirline = new Flight("b", "Other Air", "OA 1", "DEL", "Delhi", "DXB", "Dubai",
                Instant.parse("2030-01-01T11:00:00Z"), Instant.parse("2030-01-01T13:00:00Z"),
                new BigDecimal("900"), "INR", 10, "SCHEDULED", "DEMO");
        when(airportRepository.findById("DEL")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DEL", "IN")));
        when(airportRepository.findById("DXB")).thenReturn(Optional.of(airport("DXB", "AE")));
        when(repository.findByOriginCodeIgnoreCaseAndDestinationCodeIgnoreCase("DEL", "DXB"))
                .thenReturn(List.of(airIndia, otherAirline));

        assertEquals(List.of(airIndia), service.search(
                "DEL", "DXB", null, "price", "international", null, "Air India", new BigDecimal("1500")));
    }

    @Test
    void generalFlightBrowseIsBoundedToUpcomingResults() {
        List<Flight> upcoming = IntStream.range(0, 45)
                .mapToObj(index -> flight("browse-" + index, "DEL", "BOM", "SCHEDULED"))
                .toList();
        when(repository.findUpcomingForBrowsing(any(), any(), any(Pageable.class))).thenReturn(upcoming);

        List<Flight> results = service.search(null, null, null, "departure", null, null, null, null);

        assertEquals(40, results.size());
        assertTrue(results.stream().allMatch(flight -> flight.dataSource().equals("DEMO")));
    }

    @Test
    void filteredGeneralBrowseDoesNotDiscardFlightsOutsideTheUnfilteredPage() {
        Flight match = flight("filtered", "DEL", "BOM", "SCHEDULED");
        when(repository.findAll()).thenReturn(List.of(match));

        assertEquals(List.of(match), service.search(null, null, null, "departure", null, null, "Airline", null));
    }

    @Test
    void createsAndPersistsDemoFlightsForAnUnseededRouteAndDate() {
        Airport amd = airport("AMD", "IN");
        Airport hyd = airport("HYD", "IN");
        java.time.LocalDate date = java.time.LocalDate.now().plusDays(2);
        List<Flight> generated = IntStream.range(0, RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.DEMO_DEPARTURES_PER_DAY)
                .mapToObj(index -> new Flight("demo-route-AMD-HYD-" + date + "-" + (index + 1),
                        "JetSetGo Demonstration Air", "JG " + (1001 + index), "AMD", "Ahmedabad", "HYD", "Hyderabad",
                        date.atTime(8 + index * 2, 0).atZone(java.time.ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata")).toInstant(),
                        date.atTime(10 + index * 2, 0).atZone(java.time.ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata")).toInstant(),
                        new BigDecimal("5000"), "INR", 48, "SCHEDULED", "DEMO",
                        "Asia/Kolkata", "Asia/Kolkata", "Illustrative demo aircraft", null, null, null, false))
                .toList();
        List<Flight> existing = generated.subList(0, 3);
        when(airportRepository.findById("AMD")).thenReturn(Optional.of(amd));
        when(airportRepository.findById("HYD")).thenReturn(Optional.of(hyd));
        when(repository.findByOriginCodeIgnoreCaseAndDestinationCodeIgnoreCase("AMD", "HYD"))
                .thenReturn(existing).thenReturn(generated);
        when(schedules.ensureDemoRouteInstances(amd, hyd, date)).thenReturn(generated);

        List<Flight> results = service.search("AMD", "HYD", date, "departure", "domestic", "IN", null, null);

        assertEquals(generated, results);
        org.mockito.Mockito.verify(schedules).ensureDemoRouteInstances(amd, hyd, date);
    }

    private static Flight flight(String id, String origin, String destination, String status) {
        return new Flight(id, "Airline", id, origin, origin, destination, destination,
                Instant.parse("2030-01-01T10:00:00Z"), Instant.parse("2030-01-01T12:00:00Z"),
                new BigDecimal("1000"), "INR", 10, status, "DEMO");
    }

    private static Airport airport(String code, String countryCode) {
        return new Airport(code, code, code + " airport", "", countryCode, countryCode,
                "Asia/Kolkata", true, "large_airport", null, null, "TEST");
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/PaymentProcessorFacadeTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class PaymentProcessorFacadeTest {
    @Mock RazorpayService razorpay;
    @Mock DemoPaymentService demoPayments;
    @InjectMocks PaymentProcessorFacade payments;

    @Test
    void routesRefundToTheProcessorThatRecordedThePayment() {
        Booking demo = booking(null);
        Booking razorpayBooking = booking("pay_test_123");

        payments.refund(demo);
        payments.refund(razorpayBooking);

        verify(demoPayments).refund("booking-id");
        verify(razorpay).refund("pay_test_123", new BigDecimal("1000"), "INR");
    }

    private static Booking booking(String paymentId) {
        return new Booking("booking-id", "JSG12345", "user", "flight", "1A",
                "Passenger", "p@example.com", new BigDecimal("1000"), "INR",
                BookingStatus.CONFIRMED, null, paymentId, Instant.now(), "key");
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RazorpayServiceTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.HexFormat;
import java.util.Optional;
import javax.crypto.Mac;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.FindAndModifyOptions;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.web.client.RestClient;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Booking;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.BookingStatus;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.BookingRepository;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class RazorpayServiceTest {
    @Mock BookingRepository bookings;
    @Mock MongoTemplate mongo;
    @Mock RestClient.Builder restClientBuilder;
    @Mock RestClient restClient;

    @Test
    void acceptsOnlySignatureForMatchingOrderAndPayment() throws Exception {
        String secret = "test-secret";
        String orderId = "order_123";
        String paymentId = "pay_456";
        Mac mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256");
        mac.init(new SecretKeySpec(secret.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), "HmacSHA256"));
        String signature = HexFormat.of().formatHex(
                mac.doFinal((orderId + "|" + paymentId).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));

        assertTrue(RazorpayService.isValidSignature(orderId, paymentId, signature, secret));
        assertFalse(RazorpayService.isValidSignature(orderId, "pay_changed", signature, secret));
        assertFalse(RazorpayService.isValidSignature(orderId, paymentId, "not-a-signature", secret));
        assertFalse(RazorpayService.isValidSignature(orderId, paymentId, null, secret));
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotCallProviderWhenExpiryAlreadyWonThePaymentTransition() {
        Booking pending = new Booking("booking", "JSG12345", "user", "flight", "2A",
                "A Passenger", "passenger@example.com", new BigDecimal("5000"), "INR",
                BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, "order_123", null, Instant.now(), "create-key",
                null, Instant.now().plusSeconds(300));
        when(bookings.findById("booking")).thenReturn(Optional.of(pending));
        when(restClientBuilder.baseUrl("https://api.razorpay.com/v1")).thenReturn(restClientBuilder);
        when(restClientBuilder.build()).thenReturn(restClient);
        when(mongo.findAndModify(any(Query.class), any(Update.class), any(FindAndModifyOptions.class),
                eq(Booking.class))).thenReturn(null);
        RazorpayService service = new RazorpayService("rzp_test_example", "test-secret", "RAZORPAY_TEST",
                restClientBuilder, bookings, mongo);

        ResponseStatusException failure = assertThrows(ResponseStatusException.class,
                () -> service.verify("user", "booking", "order_123", "pay_456", "signature"));

        assertEquals(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, failure.getStatusCode());
        verify(restClient, never()).get();
    }

    @Test
    void refusesLiveKeyIdsInTestModeBeforeCreatingAnOrder() {
        Booking pending = new Booking("booking", "JSG12345", "user", "flight", "2A",
                "A Passenger", "passenger@example.com", new BigDecimal("5000"), "INR",
                BookingStatus.PENDING_PAYMENT, null, null, Instant.now(), "create-key",
                null, Instant.now().plusSeconds(300));
        when(bookings.findById("booking")).thenReturn(Optional.of(pending));
        when(restClientBuilder.baseUrl("https://api.razorpay.com/v1")).thenReturn(restClientBuilder);
        when(restClientBuilder.build()).thenReturn(restClient);
        RazorpayService service = new RazorpayService("rzp_live_example", "live-secret", "RAZORPAY_TEST",
                restClientBuilder, bookings, mongo);

        ResponseStatusException failure = assertThrows(ResponseStatusException.class,
                () -> service.createOrder("user", "booking"));

        assertEquals(HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, failure.getStatusCode());
        verify(bookings, never()).save(any(Booking.class));
        verify(restClient, never()).post();
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RazorpayServiceTest.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RollingFlightScheduleGeneratorTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.BulkOperations;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.MongoTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Query;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.core.query.Update;

import com.jetsetgo.domain.Flight;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.RouteTemplate;
import com.jetsetgo.repository.RouteTemplateRepository;

class RollingFlightScheduleGeneratorTest {
    private final RouteTemplateRepository templates = org.mockito.Mockito.mock(RouteTemplateRepository.class);
    private final MongoTemplate mongo = org.mockito.Mockito.mock(MongoTemplate.class);
    private final BulkOperations writes = org.mockito.Mockito.mock(BulkOperations.class);
    private final RollingFlightScheduleGenerator generator =
            new RollingFlightScheduleGenerator(templates, mongo);

    @BeforeEach
    void setUpBulkOperations() {
        org.mockito.Mockito.when(mongo.bulkOps(BulkOperations.BulkMode.UNORDERED, Flight.class)).thenReturn(writes);
        org.mockito.Mockito.when(writes.upsert(org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any(Query.class),
                org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any(Update.class))).thenReturn(writes);
    }

    @Test
    void createsStableOvernightInstanceUsingBothIanaTimezones() {
        RouteTemplate route = new RouteTemplate("route-del-sin", "Demo Air", "JG 1",
                "DEL", "Delhi", "SIN", "Singapore", "23:30", 250, List.of(1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7),
                "Asia/Kolkata", "Asia/Singapore", new BigDecimal("1000"), "INR", 48,
                "Illustrative aircraft", true);
        LocalDate departureDate = LocalDate.of(2030, 1, 2);

        Flight first = generator.createInstance(route, departureDate);
        Flight second = generator.createInstance(route, departureDate);

        assertEquals("route-del-sin:2030-01-02", first.id());
        assertEquals(first.id(), second.id());
        assertEquals("2030-01-03T06:10", first.arrival().atZone(java.time.ZoneId.of("Asia/Singapore"))
                .toLocalDateTime().toString());
        assertEquals("2030-01-02T23:30", first.departure().atZone(java.time.ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata"))
                .toLocalDateTime().toString());
    }

    @Test
    void createsThreeDailyDemoFlightsForAnUnseededScheduledRoute() {
        Airport amd = new Airport("AMD", "Ahmedabad", "Ahmedabad Airport", "Gujarat",
                "India", "IN", null, true, "large_airport", 23.0772, 72.6347, "TEST");
        Airport hyd = new Airport("HYD", "Hyderabad", "Hyderabad Airport", "Telangana",
                "India", "IN", null, true, "large_airport", 17.2313, 78.4299, "TEST");
        LocalDate date = LocalDate.now(java.time.ZoneId.of("Asia/Kolkata")).plusDays(2);

        List<Flight> generated = generator.ensureDemoRouteInstances(amd, hyd, date);

        assertEquals(RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.DEMO_DEPARTURES_PER_DAY, generated.size());
        assertEquals("AMD", generated.getFirst().originCode());
        assertEquals("HYD", generated.getFirst().destinationCode());
        assertTrue(generated.stream().allMatch(flight -> flight.dataSource().equals("DEMO")));
        assertTrue(generated.stream().allMatch(flight -> flight.status().equals("SCHEDULED")));
        assertTrue(generated.stream().allMatch(flight -> flight.departure().isBefore(flight.arrival())));
        assertTrue(generated.stream().allMatch(flight -> flight.originTimeZone().equals("Asia/Kolkata")));
        org.mockito.Mockito.verify(writes, org.mockito.Mockito.times(
                RollingFlightScheduleGenerator.DEMO_DEPARTURES_PER_DAY))
                .upsert(org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any(Query.class), org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any(Update.class));
        org.mockito.Mockito.verify(writes).execute();
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotGenerateDemoFlightsFromAirportsWithoutScheduledPassengerService() {
        Airport origin = new Airport("ABC", "Example", "Example Airport", "Region",
                "Exampleland", "XX", null, false, "small_airport", 0.0, 0.0, "TEST");
        Airport destination = new Airport("DEF", "Sample", "Sample Airport", "Region",
                "Exampleland", "XX", null, true, "medium_airport", 1.0, 1.0, "TEST");

        assertEquals(List.of(), generator.ensureDemoRouteInstances(
                origin, destination, LocalDate.now().plusDays(1)));
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/RollingFlightScheduleGeneratorTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class SeatInventoryServiceTest {
    @Test
    void assignsConfiguredCabinBlocksWithoutUnnecessarilyExpandingSmallAircraft() {
        assertEquals("FIRST", SeatInventoryService.cabinClass(0, 48));
        assertEquals("BUSINESS", SeatInventoryService.cabinClass(6, 48));
        assertEquals("PREMIUM_ECONOMY", SeatInventoryService.cabinClass(18, 48));
        assertEquals("ECONOMY", SeatInventoryService.cabinClass(24, 48));
        assertEquals("ECONOMY", SeatInventoryService.cabinClass(0, 18));
        assertEquals("ECONOMY", SeatInventoryService.cabinClass(0, 24));
        assertEquals("ECONOMY", SeatInventoryService.cabinClass(24, 30));
    }
}

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/FarePolicyResolverTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.service.fare;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

class FarePolicyResolverTest {
    private final FarePolicyResolver fares = new FarePolicyResolver(List.of(
            new EconomyFarePolicy(), new PremiumEconomyFarePolicy(),
            new BusinessFarePolicy(), new FirstClassFarePolicy()));

    @Test
    void dispatchesClassSpecificFareStrategies() {
        BigDecimal baseFare = new BigDecimal("1000");

        assertEquals(new BigDecimal("1000.00"), fares.calculate("ECONOMY", baseFare, 1));
        assertEquals(new BigDecimal("1500.00"), fares.calculate("premium_economy", baseFare, 1));
        assertEquals(new BigDecimal("5000.00"), fares.calculate("BUSINESS", baseFare, 2));
        assertEquals(new BigDecimal("4000.00"), fares.calculate("FIRST", baseFare, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsUnsupportedCabins() {
        assertThrows(ResponseStatusException.class, () -> fares.resolve("STUDIO"));
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/service/fare/FarePolicyResolverTest.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/web/AirportSecurityTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import java.util.List;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.autoconfigure.web.servlet.WebMvcTest;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.test.context.bean.override.mockito.MockitoBean;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;

import com.jetsetgo.config.JwtAuthenticationFilter;
import com.jetsetgo.config.JwtService;
import com.jetsetgo.config.SecurityConfig;
import com.jetsetgo.domain.Airport;
import com.jetsetgo.service.AirportService;

@WebMvcTest(AirportController.class)
@Import({SecurityConfig.class, JwtAuthenticationFilter.class})
class AirportSecurityTest {
    @Autowired MockMvc mvc;
    @MockitoBean AirportService airports;
    @MockitoBean JwtService jwtService;

    @Test
    void airportSearchIsPublicAndReturnsMatchingAirports() throws Exception {
        when(airports.search("HY", null, false)).thenReturn(List.of(new Airport(
                "HYD", "Hyderabad", "Rajiv Gandhi International Airport", "India", "Asia/Kolkata")));

        mvc.perform(get("/api/airports").param("q", "HY"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[0].code").value("HYD"));

        verify(airports).search("HY", null, false);
    }

    @Test
    void airportCountryCatalogIsPublic() throws Exception {
        when(airports.countryCodes()).thenReturn(List.of("IN", "US"));

        mvc.perform(get("/api/airports/countries"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$[0]").value("IN"));

        verify(airports).countryCodes();
    }

    @Test
    void bookingEndpointsRemainProtected() throws Exception {
        mvc.perform(get("/api/bookings")).andExpect(status().is4xxClientError());
    }
}

// ===== END FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/web/AirportSecurityTest.java =====

// ===== BEGIN FILE: backend/src/test/java/com/jetsetgo/web/ApiExceptionHandlerTest.java =====

package com.jetsetgo.web;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.web.server.ResponseStatusException;

class ApiExceptionHandlerTest {
    private final ApiExceptionHandler handler = new ApiExceptionHandler();

    @Test
    void exposesSafeResponseStatusReasonsAsProblemDetails() {
        var response = handler.handleResponseStatus(
                new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Passenger details are required"));

        assertEquals(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, response.getStatusCode());
        assertEquals("Passenger details are required", response.getBody().getDetail());
    }
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowDownUp, ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight, BarChart3, Check, CircleHelp, Clock3, Download,
  Globe2, LayoutDashboard, LockKeyhole, LogOut, Menu, Plane, Plus, Search, ShieldCheck, Sparkles,
  Ticket, Trash2, UserRound, Users, X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Link, Navigate, Route, Routes, useLocation, useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { api, getSession, saveSession } from "./api";
import AirportCombobox from "./components/AirportCombobox";
import type { AdminSummary, Airport, Booking, Flight, Order, RouteTemplate, Session, UserSummary } from "./types";

declare global {
  interface Window {
    Razorpay?: new (options: Record<string, unknown>) => { open: () => void };
  }
}

const money = (amount: number, currency = "INR") =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-IN", { style: "currency", currency, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(amount);
const clock = (value: string, timeZone?: string | null) => new Date(value).toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", {
  hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", ...(timeZone ? { timeZone } : {}),
});
const day = (value: string, timeZone?: string | null) => new Date(value).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
  weekday: "short", day: "numeric", month: "short", ...(timeZone ? { timeZone } : {}),
});
const displayCountry = (code: string) =>
  new Intl.DisplayNames(["en"], { type: "region" }).of(code) ?? code;
const offsetDate = (value: string, days: number) => {
  const date = new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`);
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + days);
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};
const localDateInput = (offsetDays = 0) => {
  const date = new Date();
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  date.setDate(date.getDate() + offsetDays);
  date.setMinutes(date.getMinutes() - date.getTimezoneOffset());
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};
const toLocalDateTimeInput = (value: string) => {
  const date = new Date(value);
  date.setMinutes(date.getMinutes() - date.getTimezoneOffset());
  return date.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
};
const matchesTimeWindow = (flight: Flight, window: string) => {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
    timeZone: flight.originTimeZone ?? undefined,
    hour: "2-digit",
    hourCycle: "h23",
  }).formatToParts(new Date(flight.departure));
  const hour = Number(parts.find((part) => part.type === "hour")?.value ?? 0);
  return window === "morning" ? hour < 12 : window === "afternoon" ? hour >= 12 && hour < 18 : hour >= 18;
};

export default function App() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(() => getSession());
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const updateSession = (next: Session | null) => {
    saveSession(next);
    setSession(next);
  };
  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <header className="site-header">
        <Link to="/" className="brand" aria-label="Jet Set Go home" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>
          <BrandLockup />
        </Link>
        <button className="mobile-toggle icon-button" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)} aria-label="Toggle menu">
          {mobileOpen ? <X /> : <Menu />}
        </button>
        <nav className={mobileOpen ? "main-nav is-open" : "main-nav"}>
          <Link to="/flights" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>Explore flights</Link>
          <Link to="/bookings" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>My trips</Link>
          {session?.user.role === "ADMIN" && <Link to="/admin" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>Admin</Link>}
          <a href="#why-us" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>Why Jet Set Go</a>
          {session ? (
            <button className="nav-account" onClick={() => updateSession(null)}>
              <span className="avatar">{session.user.name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}</span>
              <span>{session.user.name.split(" ")[0]}</span><LogOut size={15} />
            </button>
          ) : <Link className="nav-login" to="/login" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}><UserRound size={17} /> Log in</Link>}
        </nav>
      </header>
      <main>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
          <Route path="/flights" element={<FlightsPage />} />
          <Route path="/flight/:id" element={<FlightPage session={session} />} />
          <Route path="/login" element={session ? <Navigate to={session.user.role === "ADMIN" ? "/admin" : "/bookings"} replace /> : <AuthPage onSession={updateSession} />} />
          <Route path="/bookings" element={session ? <BookingsPage /> : <LoginPrompt />} />
          <Route path="/admin" element={session?.user.role === "ADMIN" ? <AdminPage /> : <AccessDenied />} />
          <Route path="/checkout/:id" element={session ? <CheckoutPage /> : <LoginPrompt />} />
          <Route path="/ticket/:id" element={session ? <TicketPage /> : <LoginPrompt />} />
          <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
        </Routes>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

function HomePage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [from, setFrom] = useState<Airport | null>(null);
  const [to, setTo] = useState<Airport | null>(null);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"domestic" | "international">("domestic");
  const [countryCode, setCountryCode] = useState("IN");
  const [countryCodes, setCountryCodes] = useState<string[]>(["IN"]);
  const [countryError, setCountryError] = useState("");
  const [departureDate, setDepartureDate] = useState(() => localDateInput(2));
  const [tripType, setTripType] = useState<"ONE_WAY" | "ROUND_TRIP">("ONE_WAY");
  const [returnDate, setReturnDate] = useState(() => localDateInput(7));
  const [passengerCount, setPassengerCount] = useState(1);
  const [travelClass, setTravelClass] = useState("ECONOMY");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    api.airportCountries().then((codes) => {
      setCountryCodes(codes.length ? codes : ["IN"]);
      setCountryError("");
    }).catch(() => setCountryError("Airport country list is unavailable."));
  }, []);
  const swap = () => { setFrom(to); setTo(from); setError(""); };
  const search = (origin: Airport, destination: Airport, date: string) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      from: origin.code,
      to: destination.code,
      mode,
      tripType,
      passengers: String(passengerCount),
      travelClass,
      ...(mode === "domestic" ? { countryCode } : {}),
    });
    if (date) params.set("date", date);
    if (tripType === "ROUND_TRIP") params.set("returnDate", returnDate);
    navigate(`/flights?${params}`);
  };
  return (
    <>
      <section className="hero">
        <div className="hero-grid">
          <div className="hero-copy">
            <div className="eyebrow"><span className="eyebrow-line" /> YOUR NEXT CHAPTER STARTS HERE</div>
            <h1>The world is<br />closer than <em>ever.</em></h1>
            <p>Find the flight that feels right. Thoughtfully chosen routes, transparent fares, and a smoother way to get there.</p>
            <div className="hero-proof"><span><ShieldCheck size={16} /> Secure booking</span><span><Sparkles size={16} /> Curated journeys</span></div>
          </div>
          <div className="hero-stamp"><Plane size={22} /><span>MADE FOR<br />THE WAY YOU GO</span></div>
        </div>
        <div className="search-panel">
          <div className="search-label"><span>01</span> PLAN YOUR JOURNEY <b className="demo-data-pill">DEMO DATA · NOT LIVE</b></div>
          <div className="flight-mode-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Flight type">
            <button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={mode === "domestic"} className={mode === "domestic" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => { setMode("domestic"); setFrom(null); setTo(null); setError(""); }}>Domestic flights</button>
            <button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={mode === "international"} className={mode === "international" ? "active" : ""}
              onClick={() => { setMode("international"); setFrom(null); setTo(null); setError(""); }}>International flights</button>
          </div>
          <form className={`search-form ${mode === "domestic" ? "has-country" : ""}`} onSubmit={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            if (!from || !to) { setError("Choose an origin and destination airport to search."); return; }
            if (from.code === to.code) { setError("Origin and destination must be different airports."); return; }
            if (mode === "domestic" && (from.countryCode !== countryCode || to.countryCode !== countryCode)) {
              setError("Choose both airports within the selected country."); return;
            }
            if (mode === "international" && from.countryCode === to.countryCode) {
              setError("International flights must connect airports in different countries."); return;
            }
            if (tripType === "ROUND_TRIP" && returnDate <= departureDate) {
              setError("Return date must be after the departure date."); return;
            }
            setError("");
            search(from, to, departureDate);
          }}>
            {mode === "domestic" && <label className="search-field country-select"><span>DOMESTIC COUNTRY</span>
              <select aria-label="Domestic country" value={countryCode} onChange={(event) => {
                setCountryCode(event.target.value); setFrom(null); setTo(null); setError("");
              }}>
                {countryCodes.map((code) => <option key={code} value={code}>{displayCountry(code)}</option>)}
              </select>
            </label>}
            <AirportCombobox label="FROM" selected={from} countryCode={mode === "domestic" ? countryCode : undefined}
              excludeCode={to?.code} onChange={setFrom} />
            <button type="button" className="swap-button" onClick={swap} aria-label="Swap origin and destination"><ArrowDownUp size={17} /></button>
            <AirportCombobox label="TO" selected={to} countryCode={mode === "domestic" ? countryCode : undefined}
              excludeCode={from?.code} onChange={setTo} />
            <label className="search-field search-date"><span>DEPARTURE</span><input type="date" min={localDateInput()} value={departureDate} onChange={(event) => {
              const nextDate = event.target.value;
              setDepartureDate(nextDate);
              if (returnDate <= nextDate) {
                setReturnDate(offsetDate(nextDate, 1));
              }
            }} aria-label="Departure date" /></label>
            <button className="search-submit" type="submit"><Search size={18} /><span>Find flights</span><ArrowRight size={17} /></button>
          </form>
          <div className="journey-options">
            <label><span>TRIP</span><select value={tripType} onChange={(event) => setTripType(event.target.value as "ONE_WAY" | "ROUND_TRIP")}><option value="ONE_WAY">One way</option><option value="ROUND_TRIP">Round trip</option></select></label>
            {tripType === "ROUND_TRIP" && <label><span>RETURN</span><input type="date" min={offsetDate(departureDate, 1)} value={returnDate} onChange={(event) => setReturnDate(event.target.value)} /></label>}
            <label><span>PASSENGERS</span><select value={passengerCount} onChange={(event) => setPassengerCount(Number(event.target.value))}>{Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, index) => index + 1).map((count) => <option key={count} value={count}>{count} {count === 1 ? "passenger" : "passengers"}</option>)}</select></label>
            <label><span>TRAVEL CLASS</span><select value={travelClass} onChange={(event) => setTravelClass(event.target.value)}><option value="ECONOMY">Economy</option><option value="PREMIUM_ECONOMY">Premium economy</option><option value="BUSINESS">Business</option><option value="FIRST">First</option></select></label>
          </div>
          {error && <div className="search-error" role="alert">{error}</div>}
          {countryError && <div className="search-error" role="status">{countryError} Domestic search currently defaults to India.</div>}
          <div className="search-foot"><span><LockKeyhole size={13} /> Your journey, your way</span><span>DEMO SCHEDULES · NOT LIVE FLIGHT DATA</span></div>
        </div>
        <div className="hero-orbit orbit-one" /><div className="hero-orbit orbit-two" />
      </section>
      <DemoFlightsSection />
      <section className="intro-section" id="why-us">
        <div><span className="eyebrow muted"><span className="eyebrow-line" /> A LITTLE MORE HUMAN</span><h2>Travel should feel<br />like <em>the beginning.</em></h2></div>
        <div className="intro-right"><p>From the first search to the final boarding call, we make the details feel easy—so you can focus on the moments waiting at the other end.</p><Link to="/flights" className="text-link">Explore all flights <ArrowUpRight size={16} /></Link></div>
      </section>
      <section className="destinations-section">
        <div className="destination-heading">        <div><span className="eyebrow muted"><span className="eyebrow-line" /> A FEW GOOD WAYS TO GET AWAY</span><h2>Somewhere is <em>calling.</em></h2></div><p>Explore sample routes from our demo schedule. Fares are illustrative, not live offers.</p></div>
        <div className="destination-grid">
          {[
            { from: "DEL", to: "BOM", city: "Mumbai", eyebrow: "CITY LIGHTS", detail: "A quick escape from Delhi", style: "destination-mumbai" },
            { from: "BOM", to: "BLR", city: "Bengaluru", eyebrow: "GARDEN CITY", detail: "Mumbai to Bengaluru", style: "destination-bengaluru" },
            { from: "BLR", to: "DEL", city: "New Delhi", eyebrow: "A CHANGE OF SCENE", detail: "Bengaluru to the capital", style: "destination-delhi" },
            { from: "DEL", to: "BLR", city: "Bengaluru", eyebrow: "SOUTHBOUND", detail: "A direct sample route", style: "destination-south" },
          ].map((route) => (
            <Link key={`${route.from}-${route.to}`} className={`destination-card ${route.style}`} to={`/flights?from=${route.from}&to=${route.to}`}>
              <span className="destination-code">{route.from} <ArrowRight size={14} /> {route.to}</span>
              <span className="destination-copy"><small>{route.eyebrow}</small><b>{route.city}</b><span>{route.detail}</span></span>
              <ArrowUpRight className="destination-arrow" size={18} />
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className="values-grid">
        <article className="value-card value-blue"><span className="value-icon"><Globe2 /></span><span className="value-number">01 / DISCOVER</span><h3>Good routes.<br />Better stories.</h3><p>Thoughtful connections between the places that matter to you.</p><Link to="/flights">Find your route <ArrowRight size={16} /></Link></article>
        <article className="value-card value-sand"><span className="value-icon"><Ticket /></span><span className="value-number">02 / BOOK</span><h3>Simple from<br />the start.</h3><p>Clear pricing and a booking experience that respects your time.</p><Link to="/flights">See available seats <ArrowRight size={16} /></Link></article>
        <article className="value-card value-green"><span className="value-icon"><CircleHelp /></span><span className="value-number">03 / GO</span><h3>Here for<br />the whole trip.</h3><p>Your itinerary and ticket are right where you need them.</p><Link to="/bookings">View my trips <ArrowRight size={16} /></Link></article>
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

function DemoFlightsSection() {
  const [flights, setFlights] = useState<Flight[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [warming, setWarming] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [requestVersion, setRequestVersion] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);
    setWarming(false);
    setError("");
    const warmingTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (active) setWarming(true);
    }, 2500);
    api.featuredFlights()
      .then((items) => { if (active) setFlights(items); })
      .catch((failure: Error) => { if (active) setError(failure.message); })
      .finally(() => {
        window.clearTimeout(warmingTimeout);
        if (active) {
          setLoading(false);
          setWarming(false);
        }
      });
    return () => {
      active = false;
      window.clearTimeout(warmingTimeout);
    };
  }, [requestVersion]);

  return (
    <section className="demo-flights-section">
      <div className="demo-flights-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="eyebrow muted"><span className="eyebrow-line" /> SAMPLE SCHEDULES · NOT LIVE INVENTORY</span>
          <h2>Demo flights, ready to explore.</h2>
        </div>
        <Link to="/flights" className="text-link">Browse demo flights <ArrowRight size={16} /></Link>
      </div>
      <p className="demo-flights-note">These simulated flights and fares are for demonstration only. No real airline inventory is sold.</p>
      {loading ? <div className="loading-state" role="status"><span className="spinner" />
        {warming ? "Waking the flight service; this may take a moment…" : "Loading demo flights…"}
      </div> : error ? (
        <div className="demo-flights-empty" role="alert">
          {error}{" "}
          <button type="button" className="airport-retry" onClick={() => setRequestVersion((version) => version + 1)}>
            Retry
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : flights.length ? (
        <div className="flight-list">
          {flights.map((flight) => <FlightCard key={flight.id} flight={flight} searchQuery="" />)}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="demo-flights-empty">
          Demo schedules are not available yet. Please try again after the backend finishes initializing.
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function FlightsPage() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [flights, setFlights] = useState<Flight[]>([]);
  const [airlines, setAirlines] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [warming, setWarming] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [requestVersion, setRequestVersion] = useState(0);
  const from = params.get("from") ?? "";
  const to = params.get("to") ?? "";
  const date = params.get("date") ?? "";
  const sort = params.get("sort") ?? "departure";
  const mode = params.get("mode") ?? "";
  const countryCode = params.get("countryCode") ?? "";
  const tripType = params.get("tripType") ?? "ONE_WAY";
  const returnDate = params.get("returnDate") ?? "";
  const passengerCount = params.get("passengers") ?? "1";
  const travelClass = (params.get("travelClass") ?? "ECONOMY").replace("_", " ");
  const airline = params.get("airline") ?? "";
  const maxPrice = params.get("maxPrice") ?? "";
  const timeWindow = params.get("timeWindow") ?? "";
  const maxDuration = params.get("maxDuration") ?? "";
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);
    setWarming(false);
    setError("");
    const warmingTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (active) setWarming(true);
    }, 2500);
    api.flights(from, to, date, sort, mode, countryCode)
      .then((items) => {
        if (active) {
          setFlights(items);
          setAirlines([...new Set(items.map((flight) => flight.airline))].sort());
        }
      }).catch((e: Error) => {
        if (active) setError(e.message);
      }).finally(() => {
        window.clearTimeout(warmingTimeout);
        if (active) {
          setLoading(false);
          setWarming(false);
        }
      });
    return () => {
      active = false;
      window.clearTimeout(warmingTimeout);
    };
  }, [from, to, date, sort, mode, countryCode, requestVersion]);
  const visibleFlights = flights.filter((flight) =>
    (!airline || flight.airline === airline)
    && (!maxPrice || flight.price <= Number(maxPrice))
    && (!maxDuration || (new Date(flight.arrival).getTime() - new Date(flight.departure).getTime()) / 60000 <= Number(maxDuration))
    && (!timeWindow || matchesTimeWindow(flight, timeWindow)));
  const dateStripStart = date || localDateInput();
  return (
    <section className="content-page">
      <div className="page-kicker"><span className="eyebrow-line" /> {mode ? `${mode.toUpperCase()} SEARCH` : "ALL ROUTES"} · DEMO SCHEDULES · NOT LIVE</div>
      <div className="page-heading-row"><div><h1>{from || to ? `${from || "Anywhere"} to ${to || "everywhere"}` : "Flights worth looking forward to."}</h1><p>{date ? `Sample flights for ${new Date(`${date}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long" })}.` : "Choose a flight and we’ll take it from here."} · {tripType === "ROUND_TRIP" && returnDate ? `Return ${returnDate} · ` : ""}{passengerCount} passenger{passengerCount === "1" ? "" : "s"} · {travelClass}.</p></div><div className="flight-tools">
        <label className="sort-control">Sort by<select value={sort} onChange={(event) => { const next = new URLSearchParams(params); next.set("sort", event.target.value); setParams(next); }}><option value="departure">Earliest departure</option><option value="arrival">Earliest arrival</option><option value="price">Lowest fare</option><option value="duration">Shortest duration</option></select></label>
        <label className="sort-control">Airline<select value={airline} onChange={(event) => { const next = new URLSearchParams(params); event.target.value ? next.set("airline", event.target.value) : next.delete("airline"); setParams(next); }}><option value="">All airlines</option>{airlines.map((item) => <option key={item}>{item}</option>)}</select></label>
        <label className="sort-control">Max fare<input aria-label="Maximum fare" type="number" min="0" placeholder="Any" value={maxPrice} onChange={(event) => { const next = new URLSearchParams(params); event.target.value ? next.set("maxPrice", event.target.value) : next.delete("maxPrice"); setParams(next); }} /></label>
        <label className="sort-control">Departure time<select value={timeWindow} onChange={(event) => { const next = new URLSearchParams(params); event.target.value ? next.set("timeWindow", event.target.value) : next.delete("timeWindow"); setParams(next); }}><option value="">Any time</option><option value="morning">Morning · before noon</option><option value="afternoon">Afternoon · noon–6 PM</option><option value="evening">Evening · after 6 PM</option></select></label>
        <label className="sort-control">Max duration<select value={maxDuration} onChange={(event) => { const next = new URLSearchParams(params); event.target.value ? next.set("maxDuration", event.target.value) : next.delete("maxDuration"); setParams(next); }}><option value="">Any duration</option><option value="180">3 hours</option><option value="360">6 hours</option><option value="600">10 hours</option></select></label>
        <Link to="/" className="outline-button">Edit search <ArrowUpRight size={15} /></Link>
      </div></div>
      {from && to && <nav className="date-strip" aria-label="Nearby departure dates">
        {Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, index) => {
          const optionDate = offsetDate(dateStripStart, index);
          const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
          next.set("date", optionDate);
          return <Link key={optionDate} to={`/flights?${next.toString()}`} className={optionDate === dateStripStart ? "active" : ""}>
            <small>{new Date(`${optionDate}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", { weekday: "short" })}</small>
            <b>{new Date(`${optionDate}T12:00:00`).toLocaleDateString("en-IN", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}</b>
          </Link>;
        })}
      </nav>}
      {loading ? <div className="loading-state" role="status"><span className="spinner" />
        {warming ? "Waking the flight service; this may take a moment…" : "Finding your way…"}
      </div> : error ? (
        <div className="demo-flights-empty" role="alert">
          {error}{" "}
          <button type="button" className="airport-retry" onClick={() => setRequestVersion((version) => version + 1)}>
            Retry
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : visibleFlights.length ? (
        <div className="flight-list">{visibleFlights.map((flight) => <FlightCard key={flight.id} flight={flight} searchQuery={params.toString()} />)}</div>
      ) : <div className="empty-state"><Plane size={30} /><h3>{flights.length ? "No flights match these filters" : "No demo flights for this route and date"}</h3><p>{flights.length ? "Adjust the airline, time, duration, or fare filters." : "Demo schedules are generated for airports with scheduled passenger service, up to 120 days ahead. These are simulated flights, not live airline inventory."}</p>{from && to && <Link className="text-link" to={`/flights?${new URLSearchParams({ from, to, date: offsetDate(dateStripStart, 1), mode, countryCode }).toString()}`}>Try the next day <ArrowRight size={16} /></Link>}<Link to="/" className="primary-button">Edit search <ArrowRight size={16} /></Link></div>}
    </section>
  );
}

function FlightCard({ flight, searchQuery }: { flight: Flight; searchQuery: string }) {
  return (
    <article className="flight-card">
      <div className="airline-cell"><span className="airline-icon"><Plane size={17} /></span><span><b>{flight.airline}</b><small>{flight.flightNumber} · {flight.aircraft || "Aircraft not configured"}</small></span><span className={`status-pill status-${flight.status.toLowerCase()}`}>{flight.status} · DEMO</span></div>
      <div className="route-cell"><div><b>{clock(flight.departure, flight.originTimeZone)}</b><span>{flight.originCode}</span></div><div className="route-line"><small>{day(flight.departure, flight.originTimeZone)}</small><span /><small>{Math.round((new Date(flight.arrival).getTime() - new Date(flight.departure).getTime()) / 60000)} min · Direct</small></div><div className="route-destination"><b>{clock(flight.arrival, flight.destinationTimeZone)}</b><span>{flight.destinationCode}</span></div></div>
      <div className="price-cell"><small>{flight.status === "SCHEDULED" ? "demo fare / passenger" : "not bookable"}</small><b>{money(flight.price, flight.currency)}</b><Link to={`/flight/${flight.id}${searchQuery ? `?${searchQuery}` : ""}`} className="choose-flight">{flight.status === "SCHEDULED" ? "Choose flight" : "View status"} <ArrowRight size={15} /></Link></div>
    </article>
  );
}

function FlightPage({ session }: { session: Session | null }) {
  const { id = "" } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const tripType = searchParams.get("tripType") ?? "ONE_WAY";
  const roundTrip = tripType === "ROUND_TRIP";
  const passengerCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(6, Number(searchParams.get("passengers") ?? "1") || 1));
  const travelClass = (searchParams.get("travelClass") ?? "ECONOMY").toUpperCase();
  const mode = searchParams.get("mode") ?? "";
  const countryCode = searchParams.get("countryCode") ?? "";
  const returnDate = searchParams.get("returnDate") ?? "";
  const [flight, setFlight] = useState<Flight | null>(null);
  const [isInternationalRoute, setIsInternationalRoute] = useState(mode === "international");
  const [seats, setSeats] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedSeats, setSelectedSeats] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [returnFlights, setReturnFlights] = useState<Flight[]>([]);
  const [returnFlight, setReturnFlight] = useState<Flight | null>(null);
  const [returnSeats, setReturnSeats] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedReturnSeats, setSelectedReturnSeats] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [passengers, setPassengers] = useState(() => Array.from({ length: passengerCount }, (_, index) => ({
    name: index === 0 ? session?.user.name ?? "" : "",
    nationality: "",
    passportExpiryDate: "",
  })));
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(session?.user.email ?? "");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [idempotencyKey, setIdempotencyKey] = useState(() => crypto.randomUUID());
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);
    setIsInternationalRoute(mode === "international");
    Promise.all([api.flight(id), api.seats(id, travelClass)])
      .then(async ([nextFlight, nextSeats]) => {
        const [originAirports, destinationAirports] = await Promise.all([
          api.airports(nextFlight.originCode),
          api.airports(nextFlight.destinationCode),
        ]);
        const originCountryCode = originAirports.find((airport) => airport.code === nextFlight.originCode)?.countryCode;
        const destinationCountryCode = destinationAirports.find((airport) => airport.code === nextFlight.destinationCode)?.countryCode;
        const hasCountryData = Boolean(originCountryCode && destinationCountryCode);
        const routeIsInternational = hasCountryData
          ? originCountryCode !== destinationCountryCode
          : mode === "international";
        const routeMode = hasCountryData ? routeIsInternational ? "international" : "domestic" : mode;
        const domesticCountryCode = originCountryCode || countryCode || undefined;
        const nextReturnFlights = roundTrip && returnDate
          ? await api.flights(nextFlight.destinationCode, nextFlight.originCode, returnDate,
              "departure", routeMode || undefined,
              routeMode === "domestic" ? domesticCountryCode : undefined)
          : [];
        if (!active) return;
        setFlight(nextFlight);
        setIsInternationalRoute(routeIsInternational);
        setSeats(nextSeats);
        setReturnFlights(nextReturnFlights);
        setReturnFlight(null);
        setReturnSeats([]);
        setSelectedSeats([]);
        setSelectedReturnSeats([]);
        setError("");
      })
      .catch((e: Error) => { if (active) setError(e.message); })
      .finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [id, roundTrip, returnDate, mode, countryCode, travelClass]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!returnFlight) {
      setReturnSeats([]);
      return;
    }
    let active = true;
    setReturnSeats([]);
    api.seats(returnFlight.id, travelClass)
      .then((available) => { if (active) setReturnSeats(available); })
      .catch((e: Error) => { if (active) setError(e.message); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [returnFlight?.id, travelClass]);
  useEffect(() => {
    setPassengers((current) => Array.from({ length: passengerCount }, (_, index) => current[index] ?? {
      name: "", nationality: "", passportExpiryDate: "",
    }));
  }, [passengerCount]);
  function toggleSeat(seat: string, selected: string[], update: (next: string[]) => void) {
    if (selected.includes(seat)) update(selected.filter((item) => item !== seat));
    else if (selected.length < passengerCount) update([...selected, seat]);
    setIdempotencyKey(crypto.randomUUID());
  }
  function chooseReturnFlight(nextFlight: Flight) {
    setError("");
    setReturnFlight(nextFlight);
    setSelectedReturnSeats([]);
    setIdempotencyKey(crypto.randomUUID());
  }
  function updatePassenger(index: number, field: keyof (typeof passengers)[number], value: string) {
    setPassengers((current) => current.map((passenger, passengerIndex) =>
      passengerIndex === index ? { ...passenger, [field]: value } : passenger));
    setIdempotencyKey(crypto.randomUUID());
  }
  async function submit(event: React.FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!session) { navigate("/login", { state: { from: `${location.pathname}${location.search}` } }); return; }
    if (selectedSeats.length !== passengerCount) { setError(`Choose ${passengerCount} outbound seat${passengerCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}.`); return; }
    if (roundTrip && !returnFlight) { setError("Choose a return flight to continue."); return; }
    if (roundTrip && selectedReturnSeats.length !== passengerCount) { setError(`Choose ${passengerCount} return seat${passengerCount === 1 ? "" : "s"}.`); return; }
    setBusy(true); setError("");
    try {
      const booking = await api.createBooking({
        flightId: id,
        seatNumber: selectedSeats[0],
        seatNumbers: selectedSeats,
        returnFlightId: returnFlight?.id,
        returnSeatNumbers: selectedReturnSeats,
        passengers: passengers.map((passenger) => ({
          name: passenger.name,
          ...(isInternationalRoute ? {
            nationality: passenger.nationality,
            passportExpiryDate: passenger.passportExpiryDate,
          } : {}),
        })),
        travelClass,
        passengerName: passengers[0]?.name ?? "",
        passengerEmail: email,
      }, idempotencyKey);
      navigate(`/checkout/${booking.id}`, { state: { booking } });
    } catch (e) { setError((e as Error).message); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  const classMultiplier = travelClass === "FIRST" ? 4 : travelClass === "BUSINESS" ? 2.5
    : travelClass === "PREMIUM_ECONOMY" ? 1.5 : 1;
  const estimatedTotal = flight
    ? (flight.price + (returnFlight?.price ?? 0)) * passengerCount * classMultiplier : 0;
  if (loading) return <Loading />;
  if (error && !flight) return <section className="content-page"><ErrorNote message={error} /></section>;
  if (!flight) return null;
  return (
    <section className="content-page">
      <Link to="/flights" className="back-link">← All flights</Link>
      <div className="detail-layout">
        <div>
          <div className="detail-banner"><span className="detail-airline"><Plane size={18} /> {flight.airline} · {flight.flightNumber} · {flight.dataSource === "LIVE" ? "LIVE" : "DEMO"}</span><div className="detail-route"><div><span>{flight.originCode}</span><small>{flight.originCity}</small><b>{clock(flight.departure, flight.originTimeZone)}</b></div><div className="detail-route-line"><span /> <small><Clock3 size={14} /> {Math.round((new Date(flight.arrival).getTime() - new Date(flight.departure).getTime()) / 60000)} min · non-stop</small><span /></div><div><span>{flight.destinationCode}</span><small>{flight.destinationCity}</small><b>{clock(flight.arrival, flight.destinationTimeZone)}</b></div></div><div className="detail-date">{day(flight.departure, flight.originTimeZone)} <span>·</span> {flight.currency} illustrative DEMO fare</div></div>
